```
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { alertSaying, createMember, emailLink, inviteLink, logLength, PASSWORD, signInBrowser, uniqueEmail } from "../support/journeys";

test.describe("signing in", () => {
  test("a visitor who is not signed in is sent to the sign-in page", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in/);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Sign in" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("a wrong password gets a plain message and keeps the person on the page", async ({ page }) => {
    const member = await createMember("wrongpass");
    await page.goto("/sign-in");
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(member.email);
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill("not the right password");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click();
    await expect(alertSaying(page, "The email address or password is not right. Check both and try again.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in/);
  });

  test("the right password opens the workspace, and signing out comes back to sign in", async ({ page }) => {
    const member = await createMember("signin");
    await page.goto("/sign-in");
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(member.email);
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(member.password);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: `Signed in as ${member.email}` })).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign out" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in/);
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in/); // the session really ended
  });

  test("someone already signed in is taken past the sign-in page", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("already");
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/sign-in");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/$/);
  });
});

test.describe("joining by invitation", () => {
  test("an invited person creates an account from the emailed link and ends up signed in", async ({ page }) => {
    const email = uniqueEmail("joiner");
    const link = await inviteLink(email);
    await page.goto(link);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Create your account" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Invited address")).toHaveValue(email);
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Invited address")).toHaveAttribute("readonly", "");
    expect(page.url()).not.toContain("invite="); // the token is out of the address bar
    await page.getByLabel("Your name").fill("Jo Joiner");
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill("short");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click();
    await expect(alertSaying(page, "Use at least 12 characters")).toBeVisible();
    const since = logLength();
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(PASSWORD);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Check your inbox" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.goto(await emailLink(email, "Confirm your email address", since));
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: `Signed in as ${email}` })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("a sign-up page opened without an invitation says one is needed", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/sign-up");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "You need an invitation" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.goto("/sign-up#invite=not-a-real-token&email=someone%40example.com");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "You need an invitation" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("a link that has already been used is refused, with a way forward", async ({ page, browser }) => {
    const email = uniqueEmail("twice");
    const link = await inviteLink(email);
    for (const attempt of [0, 1]) {
      const context = attempt === 0 ? page.context() : await browser.newContext();
      const tab = attempt === 0 ? page : await context.newPage();
      await tab.goto(link);
      await tab.getByLabel("Your name").fill("Twice");
      await tab.getByLabel("Password").fill(PASSWORD);
      await tab.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click();
      if (attempt === 0) await expect(tab.getByRole("heading", { name: "Check your inbox" })).toBeVisible();
      else await expect(alertSaying(tab, "Ask for a new one")).toBeVisible();
    }
  });

  test("someone who has not confirmed their address is told to, and can ask for the link again", async ({ page }) => {
    const email = uniqueEmail("unconfirmed");
    await page.goto(await inviteLink(email));
    await page.getByLabel("Your name").fill("Unconfirmed");
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(PASSWORD);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Check your inbox" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.goto("/sign-in");
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(email);
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(PASSWORD);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click();
    await expect(alertSaying(page, "Confirm your email address first")).toBeVisible();
    const since = logLength();
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send the link again" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText(`We sent a new confirmation link to ${email}.`)).toBeVisible();
    await emailLink(email, "Confirm your email address", since);
  });
});

test.describe("forgotten passwords", () => {
  test("the page answers the same for any address, and a reset link sets a new password", async ({ page }) => {
    const member = await createMember("forgetful");
    await page.goto("/forgot-password");
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill("nobody-at-all@example.com");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send the link" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Check your inbox" })).toBeVisible();

    await page.goto("/forgot-password");
    const since = logLength();
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(member.email);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send the link" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Check your inbox" })).toBeVisible();

    await page.goto(await emailLink(member.email, "Reset your password", since));
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Choose a new password" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByLabel("New password").fill("a brand new passphrase");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save new password" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in\?reset=1/);
    await expect(page.getByText("Your password was changed. Sign in with the new one.")).toBeVisible();

    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(member.email);
    await page.getByLabel("Password").fill("a brand new passphrase");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: `Signed in as ${member.email}` })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("a reset page without a valid link offers to send a new one", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/reset-password");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "This link has expired" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Send a new link" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/forgot-password/);
  });

  test("a confirmation link that has expired offers a new one", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/verify-email?error=invalid_token");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "This link has expired" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill("someone@example.com");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send a new link" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("If someone@example.com has an account waiting to be confirmed")).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

### Task 4: Talk to the Python API from the browser

Plain TypeScript with no React, so every rule is unit-tested without a browser.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/api/types.ts`, `web/src/lib/api/sse.ts`, `web/src/lib/api/token.ts`, `web/src/lib/api/client.ts`, `web/src/lib/api/format.ts`
- Create: `web/tests/api/sse.test.ts`, `web/tests/api/token.test.ts`, `web/tests/api/client.test.ts`, `web/tests/api/format.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: The API's routes and event shapes from plan 1 (`src/scraper_api/routes.py`, `schemas.py`, `events.py`).
- Produces: `parseSse(body)`; `createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now?, skewMs? })` with `get()` and `invalidate()`; `createApiClient({ baseUrl, tokens, fetchImpl? })` with `get`, `post`, `put`, `stream`, `download`; `ApiError(status, code, message, extra)`; `describeCall(name, args)`, `hostAndPath(url)`; the response types.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `web/tests/api/sse.test.ts`**

A run arrives as a server-sent event stream: `event:` and `data:` lines with a blank line between frames. The parser must cope with frames split across network chunks, several frames in one chunk, comments (keep-alives) and a stream cut off in the middle of a frame, which must be dropped, not acted on.

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseSse, SseFormatError, type SseFrame } from "../../src/lib/api/sse";

const streamOf = (...chunks: (string | Uint8Array)[]) =>
  new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    start(controller) {
      for (const chunk of chunks) controller.enqueue(typeof chunk === "string" ? new TextEncoder().encode(chunk) : chunk);
      controller.close();
    },
  });

async function collect(stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>): Promise<SseFrame[]> {
  const frames: SseFrame[] = [];
  for await (const frame of parseSse(stream)) frames.push(frame);
  return frames;
}

describe("parseSse", () => {
  it("reads a frame's event name and JSON data", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf('event: answer\ndata: {"kind":"answer","text":"Hi"}\n\n'));
    expect(frames).toEqual([{ event: "answer", data: { kind: "answer", text: "Hi" } }]);
  });

  it("reads several frames, in order, from one chunk", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf('event: a\ndata: {"n":1}\n\nevent: b\ndata: {"n":2}\n\nevent: end\ndata: {}\n\n'));
    expect(frames.map((f) => f.event)).toEqual(["a", "b", "end"]);
  });

  it("puts a frame back together when a chunk ends in the middle of it", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf("event: answer\nda", 'ta: {"text":"split', ' here"}\n', "\nevent: end\ndata: {}\n\n"));
    expect(frames).toEqual([
      { event: "answer", data: { text: "split here" } },
      { event: "end", data: {} },
    ]);
  });

  it("puts a character back together when a chunk ends in the middle of it", async () => {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode('event: answer\ndata: {"text":"café 🔑"}\n\n');
    const cut = bytes.indexOf(0xc3) + 1; // between the two bytes of é
    const frames = await collect(streamOf(bytes.slice(0, cut), bytes.slice(cut)));
    expect(frames[0].data).toEqual({ text: "café 🔑" });
  });

  it("accepts Windows line endings", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf('event: answer\r\ndata: {"text":"x"}\r\n\r\n'));
    expect(frames).toEqual([{ event: "answer", data: { text: "x" } }]);
  });

  it("ignores comment lines and unknown fields, and treats a missing event name as 'message'", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf(': keep-alive\n\nid: 7\nretry: 100\ndata: {"a":1}\n\n'));
    expect(frames).toEqual([{ event: "message", data: { a: 1 } }]);
  });

  it("joins data split over several lines", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf('event: x\ndata: {"a":\ndata: 1}\n\n'));
    expect(frames[0].data).toEqual({ a: 1 });
  });

  it("drops a half frame left when the stream is cut off", async () => {
    const frames = await collect(streamOf('event: end\ndata: {}\n\nevent: answer\ndata: {"te'));
    expect(frames.map((f) => f.event)).toEqual(["end"]);
  });

  it("refuses data that is not JSON, rather than guessing", async () => {
    await expect(collect(streamOf("event: answer\ndata: not json\n\n"))).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(SseFormatError);
  });

  it("gives nothing for an empty stream", async () => {
    expect(await collect(streamOf())).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("stops reading the stream when the consumer stops early", async () => {
    let cancelled = false;
    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      pull(controller) {
        controller.enqueue(new TextEncoder().encode('event: a\ndata: {"n":1}\n\n'));
      },
      cancel() {
        cancelled = true;
      },
    });
    for await (const frame of parseSse(stream)) {
      expect(frame.event).toBe("a");
      break;
    }
    expect(cancelled).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Create `web/tests/api/token.test.ts`**

The API wants a bearer token that lives 15 minutes. The cache reuses a token until one minute before it expires, makes one request when several callers ask at once, and forgets a token on request (the API refused it). A token fetched before `invalidate()` must not be kept.

```
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createTokenCache } from "../../src/lib/api/token";

const jwt = (exp: number | undefined) => `h.${Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(exp === undefined ? { sub: "u" } : { sub: "u", exp })).toString("base64url")}.s`;
const NOW = 1_800_000_000_000; // ms

describe("createTokenCache", () => {
  it("fetches a token once and reuses it until a minute before it expires", async () => {
    let now = NOW;
    const fetchToken = vi.fn(async () => jwt(NOW / 1000 + 900)); // good for 15 minutes
    const cache = createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now: () => now });
    const first = await cache.get();
    now += 13 * 60_000; // 13 minutes later: still more than a minute left
    expect(await cache.get()).toBe(first);
    expect(fetchToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("fetches a new one once less than a minute is left", async () => {
    let now = NOW;
    const fetchToken = vi.fn(async () => jwt(now / 1000 + 900));
    const cache = createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now: () => now });
    await cache.get();
    now += 14.5 * 60_000; // 30 seconds left
    await cache.get();
    expect(fetchToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("makes one request when several callers ask at the same moment", async () => {
    const fetchToken = vi.fn(async () => {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
      return jwt(NOW / 1000 + 900);
    });
    const cache = createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now: () => NOW });
    const tokens = await Promise.all([cache.get(), cache.get(), cache.get()]);
    expect(new Set(tokens).size).toBe(1);
    expect(fetchToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("forgets the token when told it was refused", async () => {
    const fetchToken = vi.fn(async () => jwt(NOW / 1000 + 900));
    const cache = createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now: () => NOW });
    await cache.get();
    cache.invalidate();
    await cache.get();
    expect(fetchToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("passes a failure on and tries again next time, remembering nothing", async () => {
    const fetchToken = vi.fn().mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("signed out")).mockResolvedValueOnce(jwt(NOW / 1000 + 900));
    const cache = createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now: () => NOW });
    await expect(cache.get()).rejects.toThrow("signed out");
    expect(await cache.get()).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it.each([["has no expiry", jwt(undefined)], ["is not a token at all", "garbage"], ["has a payload that is not JSON", "a.!!!.c"]])("does not keep a token that %s", async (_name, token) => {
    const fetchToken = vi.fn(async () => token);
    const cache = createTokenCache({ fetchToken, now: () => NOW });
    await cache.get();
    await cache.get();
    expect(fetchToken).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `web/tests/api/client.test.ts`**

The client turns the API's error body into one `ApiError` with a message written for people, never shows a body it cannot read, retries once with a new token after a `401`, and never retries a request the caller stopped.

```
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError, createApiClient } from "../../src/lib/api/client";

const BASE = "https://api.test";
const json = (status: number, body: unknown) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
const stream = (text: string) => new Response(text, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "text/event-stream" } });

function setup(responses: (Response | Error)[]) {
  const calls: { url: string; init: RequestInit & { headers: Record<string, string> } }[] = [];
  const fetchImpl = vi.fn(async (url: string, init: RequestInit & { headers: Record<string, string> }) => {
    calls.push({ url, init });
    const next = responses.shift();
    if (next instanceof Error) throw next;
    return next ?? json(500, {});
  });
  let issued = 0;
  const tokens = { get: vi.fn(async () => `token-${++issued}`), invalidate: vi.fn() };
  return { api: createApiClient({ baseUrl: BASE, tokens, fetchImpl: fetchImpl as unknown as typeof fetch }), calls, tokens, fetchImpl };
}

describe("createApiClient", () => {
  it("sends the token in the Authorization header and returns the JSON", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup([json(200, { ok: 1 })]);
    expect(await api.get<{ ok: number }>("/state")).toEqual({ ok: 1 });
    expect(calls[0].url).toBe(`${BASE}/state`);
    expect(calls[0].init.headers.authorization).toBe("Bearer token-1");
  });

  it("sends a JSON body with POST and PUT", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup([json(200, {}), new Response(null, { status: 204 })]);
    await api.post("/scrape", { url: "https://a.test" });
    await api.put("/settings/url", { value: "x" });
    expect(calls[0].init.method).toBe("POST");
    expect(calls[0].init.body).toBe('{"url":"https://a.test"}');
    expect(calls[0].init.headers["content-type"]).toBe("application/json");
    expect(calls[1].init.method).toBe("PUT");
  });

  it("returns undefined for an answer with no body", async () => {
    const { api } = setup([new Response(null, { status: 204 })]);
    expect(await api.post("/session/new")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("asks for a fresh token and tries once more when the first is refused", async () => {
    const { api, calls, tokens } = setup([json(401, { error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "expired" } }), json(200, { ok: 1 })]);
    expect(await api.get("/state")).toEqual({ ok: 1 });
    expect(tokens.invalidate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(calls.map((c) => c.init.headers.authorization)).toEqual(["Bearer token-1", "Bearer token-2"]);
  });

  it("gives up after one retry", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup([json(401, { error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "no" } }), json(401, { error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "still no" } })]);
    await expect(api.get("/state")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401, code: "unauthorized", message: "still no" });
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("turns the API's error body into an ApiError with its code and message", async () => {
    const { api } = setup([json(429, { error: { code: "limit", message: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day)." } })]);
    const error = await api.get("/state").catch((e) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ status: 429, code: "limit", message: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day)." });
  });

  it("keeps the extra detail the API sends, such as a fetch hint", async () => {
    const { api } = setup([json(400, { error: { code: "fetch_failed", message: "HTTP 403", hint: "Try stealthy mode.", retry_mode: "stealthy" } })]);
    const error = (await api.post("/scrape", {}).catch((e) => e)) as ApiError;
    expect(error.extra).toMatchObject({ hint: "Try stealthy mode.", retry_mode: "stealthy" });
  });

  it("copes with an error that is not JSON", async () => {
    const { api } = setup([new Response("<html>Bad gateway</html>", { status: 502 })]);
    const error = (await api.get("/state").catch((e) => e)) as ApiError;
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ status: 502, code: "server_error" });
    expect(error.message).not.toContain("<html>");
  });

  it("says plainly when the server cannot be reached", async () => {
    const { api } = setup([new TypeError("fetch failed")]);
    const error = (await api.get("/state").catch((e) => e)) as ApiError;
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ status: 0, code: "network" });
    expect(error.message).toMatch(/could not reach/i);
  });

  it("does not retry a refused request that never reached the server", async () => {
    const { api, fetchImpl } = setup([new TypeError("fetch failed")]);
    await api.get("/state").catch(() => undefined);
    expect(fetchImpl).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("streams events from a POST", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup([stream('event: answer\ndata: {"kind":"answer","text":"Hi"}\n\nevent: end\ndata: {"drafts":[]}\n\n')]);
    const frames = [];
    for await (const frame of api.stream("/agent/run", { instruction: "go", model: "m" })) frames.push(frame);
    expect(frames.map((f) => f.event)).toEqual(["answer", "end"]);
    expect(calls[0].init.method).toBe("POST");
    expect(calls[0].init.headers.accept).toBe("text/event-stream");
    expect(calls[0].init.body).toBe('{"instruction":"go","model":"m"}');
  });

  it("throws before streaming anything when the API refuses to start the stream", async () => {
    const { api } = setup([json(409, { error: { code: "busy", message: "Another run is in progress." } })]);
    const frames: unknown[] = [];
    const attempt = (async () => {
      for await (const frame of api.stream("/ask", { question: "q", model: "m" })) frames.push(frame);
    })();
    await expect(attempt).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: "busy" });
    expect(frames).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("passes the abort signal to the request, so leaving the page stops the stream", async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const { api, calls } = setup([stream("")]);
    for await (const _frame of api.stream("/ask", { question: "q" }, controller.signal)) void _frame;
    expect(calls[0].init.signal).toBe(controller.signal);
  });

  it("downloads a file with the token", async () => {
    const { api, calls } = setup([new Response("plan,price\nSolo,$9\n", { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "text/csv" } })]);
    const blob = await api.download("/rows.csv");
    expect(await blob.text()).toBe("plan,price\nSolo,$9\n");
    expect(calls[0].init.headers.authorization).toBe("Bearer token-1");
  });
});
```

```
    ["set_fetch_delay", { seconds: 5, reason: "the site is slow" }, "Waiting 5 seconds between pages: the site is slow"],
    ["ask_user", { question: "Which?" }, "Asking you a question"],
    ["something_new", {}, "something_new"],
  ])("%s", (name, args, expected) => {

  });

  it("copes with arguments that are missing or odd", () => {

    expect(describeCall("set_fetch_delay", { seconds: 1 })).toBe("Waiting 1 second between pages");

    expect(hostAndPath("https://www.shop.example/plans/")).toBe("shop.example/plans");
    expect(hostAndPath("http://shop.example/")).toBe("shop.example");
    expect(hostAndPath("https://shop.example/a?b=1#c")).toBe("shop.example/a?b=1");
  });
  it("leaves something that is not an address alone", () => {
    expect(hostAndPath("not a url")).toBe("not a url");
  });
});

describe("shorten", () => {

    expect(shorten("short", 10)).toBe("short");
    expect(shorten("x".repeat(30), 10)).toBe("xxxxxxxxxx\n[shortened]");
  });
});
```

```
/** The shapes the Python API sends. They mirror `src/scraper_api/schemas.py` and the agent's events. */

export type Usage = { day: string; model_calls: number; model_calls_limit: number; pages: number; pages_limit: number; resets_at: string };
export type PageSummary = { url: string; title: string; chars: number; links: number; looks_empty: boolean; used: boolean };
export type TraceEntry = { name: string; args: Record<string, unknown>; text: string; ok: boolean };
export type HistoryMessage = {
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  content: string;
  reasoning: string;
  sources: string[];
  interrupted: boolean;
  note: string;
  trace: TraceEntry[];
};
export type Followup = { id: string; text: string; delivered: boolean };

/** What a paused run is waiting for. */
export type AskPending = { kind: "ask"; question: string; options: string[]; interrupt_id?: string };
export type SitePending = { kind: "site"; question: string; host: string; interrupt_id?: string };
export type LimitPending = {
  kind: "limit";
  question: string;
  reason: "steps" | "pages" | "repeat";
  actions: { label: string; action: "continue" | "wrap_up" | "stop" }[];
  interrupt_id?: string;
};
export type Pending = AskPending | SitePending | LimitPending;

export type RunStatus = "running" | "paused" | "stopped" | "error" | "done" | "ended";
export type RunView = {
  number: number;
  instruction: string;
  model: string;
  status: RunStatus;
  resumable: boolean;
  pending: Pending | null;
  fetched: number;
  max_pages: number;
  delay: number;
  rows_added: number;
  trace: TraceEntry[];
  followups: Followup[];
};
export type ModelView = { id: string; context: number | null; loaded: boolean };
export type SettingName = "url" | "mode" | "follow" | "depth" | "agent";

export type WorkspaceState = {
  user: { id: string };
  provider: { key: string; name: string; hosted: boolean };
  busy: boolean;
  pages: PageSummary[];
  failed: { url: string; reason: string }[];
  rows: Record<string, unknown>[];
  history: HistoryMessage[];
  run: RunView | null;
  progress: [number, number];
  settings: Record<SettingName, string>;
  model: string | null;
  models: ModelView[];
  models_error: string | null;
  fetch_modes: string[];
  usage: Usage;
};

/** The events of a stream. `kind` is also the SSE event name. */
export type AgentEvent = {
  kind: "step" | "reasoning" | "answer" | "tool_start" | "tool_result" | "rows_added" | "needs_input" | "limit" | "error" | "done" | "followup";
  text: string;
  name: string;
  args: Record<string, unknown>;
  step: number;
  ok: boolean;
  count: number;
  urls: string[];
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  id: string;
};
export type AskEvent = { kind: "reasoning" | "answer" | "error"; text: string };
export type ServerErrorFrame = { kind: "error"; code?: string; text: string };
export type EndFrame = { drafts: string[]; leftovers: string[]; interrupted: boolean; usage: Usage };
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Create `web/src/lib/api/sse.ts`**

```
/** Reads a server-sent event stream (`event:` and `data:` lines, a blank line between frames) from a fetch response body. */

export type SseFrame = { event: string; data: unknown };

export class SseFormatError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("The server sent something that could not be read.");
  }
}

function parseFrame(raw: string): SseFrame | null {
  let event = "message";
  const data: string[] = [];
  for (const line of raw.split("\n")) {
    if (line.startsWith(":")) continue; // a comment, such as a keep-alive
    const colon = line.indexOf(":");
    const field = colon < 0 ? line : line.slice(0, colon);
    const value = colon < 0 ? "" : line.slice(colon + 1).replace(/^ /, "");
    if (field === "event") event = value;
    else if (field === "data") data.push(value);
  }
  if (data.length === 0) return null;
  try {
    return { event, data: JSON.parse(data.join("\n")) };
  } catch {
    throw new SseFormatError();
  }
}

export async function* parseSse(body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>): AsyncGenerator<SseFrame> {
  const reader = body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = "";
  try {
    for (;;) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer = (buffer + decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })).replace(/\r\n/g, "\n");
      for (let end = buffer.indexOf("\n\n"); end >= 0; end = buffer.indexOf("\n\n")) {
        const frame = parseFrame(buffer.slice(0, end));
        buffer = buffer.slice(end + 2);
        if (frame) yield frame;
      }
    }
    // Whatever is left is a frame the stream was cut off in the middle of: drop it rather than act on half a message.
  } finally {
    await reader.cancel().catch(() => undefined); // also runs when the consumer stops early, which closes the connection
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `web/src/lib/api/token.ts`**

```
/** Keeps the short-lived token for the Python API, and asks for a new one a minute before it runs out. */

export type TokenCache = { get(): Promise<string>; invalidate(): void };

/** The `exp` claim of a token in milliseconds, or null if it cannot be read. The signature is not checked: the API does that. */
function expiryOf(token: string): number | null {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(atob(token.split(".")[1].replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/"))) as { exp?: unknown };
    return typeof payload.exp === "number" ? payload.exp * 1000 : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function createTokenCache(options: { fetchToken: () => Promise<string>; now?: () => number; skewMs?: number }): TokenCache {
  const { fetchToken, now = Date.now, skewMs = 60_000 } = options;
  let cached: { token: string; expiresAt: number } | null = null;
  let inflight: Promise<string> | null = null;
  let generation = 0; // bumped by invalidate(), so a token fetched before it is not kept

  return {
    async get() {
      if (cached && cached.expiresAt - skewMs > now()) return cached.token;
      if (!inflight) {
        const mine = generation;
        const request = fetchToken()
          .then((token) => {
            const expiresAt = expiryOf(token);
            if (expiresAt !== null && mine === generation) cached = { token, expiresAt };
            return token;
          })
          .finally(() => {
            if (inflight === request) inflight = null;
          });
        inflight = request;
      }
      return inflight;
    },
    invalidate() {
      cached = null;
      inflight = null;
      generation += 1;
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Create `web/src/lib/api/client.ts`**

```
import { parseSse, type SseFrame } from "./sse";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly status: number,
    public readonly code: string,
    message: string,
    public readonly extra: Record<string, unknown> = {},
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

type Tokens = { get(): Promise<string>; invalidate(): void };
type Send = { method: string; body?: unknown; accept?: string; signal?: AbortSignal };

export type ApiClient = {
  get<T>(path: string): Promise<T>;
  post<T = void>(path: string, body?: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T>;
  put<T = void>(path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T>;
  stream(path: string, body: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): AsyncGenerator<SseFrame>;
  download(path: string): Promise<Blob>;
};

async function toError(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
  try {
    const { error } = (await response.json()) as { error?: { code?: string; message?: string; [key: string]: unknown } };
    if (error?.message) {
      const { code, message, ...extra } = error;
      return new ApiError(response.status, code ?? "error", message, extra);
    }
  } catch {
    // not JSON: fall through, and never show whatever it was
  }
  return new ApiError(response.status, "server_error", "The server had a problem. Try again in a moment.");
}

export function createApiClient(options: { baseUrl: string; tokens: Tokens; fetchImpl?: typeof fetch }): ApiClient {
  const { baseUrl, tokens, fetchImpl = fetch } = options;

  async function send(path: string, { method, body, accept, signal }: Send): Promise<Response> {
    for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
      const token = await tokens.get();
      let response: Response;
      try {
        response = await fetchImpl(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
          method,
          headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, ...(body === undefined ? {} : { "content-type": "application/json" }), ...(accept ? { accept } : {}) },
          body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
          signal,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        if (signal?.aborted) throw error; // the caller stopped it on purpose
        throw new ApiError(0, "network", "Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.");
      }
      if (response.status === 401 && attempt === 0) {
        tokens.invalidate(); // the token may simply have expired: get a fresh one and try once more
        continue;
      }
      if (!response.ok) throw await toError(response);
      return response;
    }
  }

  const json = async <T>(response: Response): Promise<T> => (response.status === 204 ? (undefined as T) : ((await response.json()) as T));

  return {
    get: async (path) => json(await send(path, { method: "GET" })),
    post: async (path, body, signal) => json(await send(path, { method: "POST", body, signal })),
    put: async (path, body) => json(await send(path, { method: "PUT", body })),
    async *stream(path, body, signal) {
      const response = await send(path, { method: "POST", body, accept: "text/event-stream", signal });
      if (!response.body) throw new ApiError(response.status, "server_error", "The server had a problem. Try again in a moment.");
      yield* parseSse(response.body);
    },
    download: async (path) => (await send(path, { method: "GET" })).blob(),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Create `web/src/lib/api/format.ts`**

```
/** Words for what the agent is doing, and small text helpers. */

/** `https://www.shop.example/plans/?a=1#top` becomes `shop.example/plans/?a=1`. Anything that is not an address is left alone. */
export function hostAndPath(url: string): string {
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(url);
    const path = parsed.pathname === "/" ? "" : parsed.pathname.replace(/\/$/, "");
    return `${parsed.host.replace(/^www\./, "")}${path}${parsed.search}`;
  } catch {
    return url;
  }
}

const plural = (count: number, word: string) => `${count} ${word}${count === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

export function describeCall(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const url = typeof args.url === "string" && args.url ? hostAndPath(args.url) : null;
  switch (name) {
    case "fetch_page":
      return url ? `Fetching ${url}${typeof args.mode === "string" && args.mode ? ` in ${args.mode} mode` : ""}` : "Fetching a page";
    case "list_links":
      return `Listing the links on ${url ?? "a page"}`;
    case "read_page":
      return `Reading ${url ?? "a page"}`;
    case "extract":
      return `Pulling ${String(args.selector ?? "")} from ${url ?? "a page"}`.replace("Pulling  from", "Pulling data from");
    case "add_rows":
      return `Adding ${plural(Array.isArray(args.rows) ? args.rows.length : 0, "row")} to the results`;
    case "set_fetch_delay": {
      const seconds = Number(args.seconds);
      const reason = typeof args.reason === "string" && args.reason ? `: ${args.reason}` : "";
      return `Waiting ${plural(Number.isFinite(seconds) ? seconds : 0, "second")} between pages${reason}`;
    }
    case "ask_user":
      return "Asking you a question";
    default:
      return name;
  }
}

export function shorten(text: string, limit = 1500): string {
  return text.length <= limit ? text : `${text.slice(0, limit).trimEnd()}\n[shortened]`;
}
```

### Task 5: Follow a run as it streams

The hardest rules of the screen, with no screen yet. A stream can end five ways (done, paused for an answer, stopped, failed, or cut off), and each must read correctly.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/workspace/live.ts`, `web/src/lib/workspace/derive.ts`
- Create: `web/tests/workspace/fixtures.ts`, `web/tests/workspace/live.test.ts`, `web/tests/workspace/derive.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: The types from Task 4 and `describeCall`.
- Produces: `liveReducer(state, action)` over `LiveTurn | null` with actions `start`, `frame`, `queued`, `failed`, `resume`, `clear`; `deriveThread(state, live)`, `deriveRunPanel(state, live)`, `isWorking(state, live)`, `summarizeWork(trace)`, `shouldKeepLive(turn)`; `CONNECTION_LOST`, `LIMIT_MESSAGES`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Create `web/tests/workspace/live.test.ts`**

`LiveTurn` is the one turn that is streaming into this screen. The reducer turns the API's events into it: steps reset the reasoning and the answer, text accumulates, each tool call becomes a log line that is completed by its result, rows add up, a question pauses the turn, and the final `end` frame decides what a stream with no outcome means (a lost connection for a run; a finished answer for a question to the pages).

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { liveReducer, type LiveAction, type LiveTurn } from "../../src/lib/workspace/live";
import { endFrame, event, makeRun } from "./fixtures";

const run = (actions: LiveAction[], from: LiveTurn | null = null) => actions.reduce<LiveTurn | null>((state, action) => liveReducer(state, action), from);
const frames = (...list: { event: string; data: unknown }[]): LiveAction[] => list.map((f) => ({ type: "frame", event: f.event, data: f.data }));
const start = (text = "Collect the plans"): LiveAction => ({ type: "start", kind: "agent", text });

describe("starting", () => {
  it("begins a streaming turn holding what the person sent", () => {
    expect(run([start()])).toMatchObject({ kind: "agent", text: "Collect the plans", status: "streaming", log: [], answer: "", reasoning: "", pending: null, ended: false });
  });

  it("can pick up a run that is already under way, with its log, its rows and the follow-ups already read", () => {
    const existing = makeRun({
      instruction: "Collect the plans",
      rows_added: 4,
      trace: [{ name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://a.test" }, text: "Fetched", ok: true }],
      followups: [{ id: "f1", text: "also the SKU", delivered: true }, { id: "f2", text: "not read yet", delivered: false }],
    });
    const turn = run([{ type: "start", kind: "agent", text: existing.instruction, from: existing }])!;
    expect(turn.log).toEqual([{ id: 1, name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://a.test" }, text: "Fetched", ok: true, done: true }]);
    expect(turn.rowsAdded).toBe(4);
    expect(turn.followups).toEqual(["also the SKU"]);
    expect(turn.queued).toEqual([{ id: "f2", text: "not read yet" }]);
  });
});

describe("the agent's steps", () => {
  it("collects reasoning and answer separately, and starts them again at each new step", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("step", { step: 1 }), event("reasoning", { text: "Hmm. " }), event("reasoning", { text: "Yes." }), event("answer", { text: "I will " }), event("answer", { text: "look." }))])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ step: 1, reasoning: "Hmm. Yes.", answer: "I will look." });
    const next = run(frames(event("step", { step: 2 })), turn)!;
    expect(next).toMatchObject({ step: 2, reasoning: "", answer: "" });
  });

  it("logs each call when it starts and fills in its result when it finishes, in order", () => {
    const turn = run([
      start(),
      ...frames(
        event("tool_start", { name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://a.test" } }),
        event("tool_start", { name: "read_page", args: { url: "https://a.test" } }),
        event("tool_result", { text: "Fetched a.test", ok: true }),
        event("tool_result", { text: "Could not read", ok: false }),
      ),
    ])!;
    expect(turn.log.map((e) => [e.name, e.done, e.ok, e.text])).toEqual([
      ["fetch_page", true, true, "Fetched a.test"],
      ["read_page", true, false, "Could not read"],
    ]);
  });

  it("ignores a result with no call waiting for it", () => {
    expect(run([start(), ...frames(event("tool_result", { text: "stray" }))])!.log).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("adds up the rows the agent records", () => {
    expect(run([start(), ...frames(event("rows_added", { count: 3 }), event("rows_added", { count: 2 }))])!.rowsAdded).toBe(5);
  });
});

describe("how a turn ends", () => {
  it("is done when the agent says so, and the end frame carries the usage", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("done"), endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "done", ended: true });
    expect(turn.usage?.model_calls).toBe(3);
  });

  it("is paused, with the question, when the agent needs an answer, and stays paused after the stream closes", () => {
    const payload = { kind: "ask", question: "Which plan?", options: ["Solo", "Team"], interrupt_id: "i1" };
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("needs_input", { payload }), endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "paused", ended: true, pending: payload });
  });

  it("is stopped when the person stopped it, and hands back the messages that were never read", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("limit", { text: "stopped" }), endFrame({ drafts: ["never mind the footer"] }))])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "stopped", drafts: ["never mind the footer"] });
  });

  it("says why the conversation ended when it no longer fits the model, and that it cannot be resumed", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("limit", { text: "context" }), endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "error", resumable: false });
    expect(turn.notice).toMatch(/no longer fits the model's context window/);
  });

  it("treats an error the server marks final as one that cannot be resumed", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("error", { text: "The model returned an empty answer.", payload: { final: true } }), endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "error", resumable: false });
  });

  it("shows an error as an error, with the words the API gave", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("error", { text: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day)." }), endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "error", notice: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day).", resumable: true });
  });

  it("says the connection was lost when the stream ends without any outcome", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames(event("answer", { text: "partial" }), endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn.status).toBe("error");
    expect(turn.notice).toMatch(/connection was lost/i);
    expect(turn.answer).toBe("partial");
  });

  it("reports a request that failed before any stream began", () => {
    expect(run([start(), { type: "failed", message: "Another run is in progress." }])).toMatchObject({ status: "error", notice: "Another run is in progress.", ended: true });
  });

  it("cannot be resumed after a request the server refused, but can after a stream that broke part-way", () => {
    expect(run([start(), { type: "failed", message: "The server is busy." }])).toMatchObject({ resumable: false });
    expect(run([start(), { type: "failed", message: "The connection was lost.", resumable: true }])).toMatchObject({ resumable: true });
  });

  it("can be continued after a pause or a stop, keeping what it had", () => {
    const paused = run([start(), ...frames(event("tool_start", { name: "fetch_page" }), event("tool_result", { text: "ok" }), event("needs_input", { payload: { kind: "ask", question: "?", options: [] } }), endFrame())])!;
    const again = run([{ type: "resume" }], paused)!;
    expect(again).toMatchObject({ status: "streaming", pending: null, ended: false, notice: null });
    expect(again.log).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe("messages sent while it works", () => {
  it("waits as queued until the agent reads it, then becomes part of the conversation", () => {
    const queued = run([start(), { type: "queued", id: "f1", text: "only the plans" }])!;
    expect(queued.queued).toEqual([{ id: "f1", text: "only the plans" }]);
    const read = run(frames(event("followup", { text: "only the plans", id: "f1" })), queued)!;
    expect(read.queued).toEqual([]);
    expect(read.followups).toEqual(["only the plans"]);
  });

  it("shows a follow-up the agent read even if this screen never saw it queued", () => {
    expect(run([start(), ...frames(event("followup", { text: "from another tab", id: "zz" }))])!.followups).toEqual(["from another tab"]);
  });
});

describe("a question about the pages", () => {
  const ask: LiveAction = { type: "start", kind: "ask", text: "What does Team cost?" };

  it("streams reasoning and answer, and finishes done", () => {
    const turn = run([ask, ...frames({ event: "reasoning", data: { kind: "reasoning", text: "Check." } }, { event: "answer", data: { kind: "answer", text: "Forty-nine dollars." } }, endFrame())])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ kind: "ask", reasoning: "Check.", answer: "Forty-nine dollars.", status: "done", ended: true });
  });

  it("keeps a partial answer when the model fails part-way, and remembers it was cut off", () => {
    const turn = run([ask, ...frames({ event: "answer", data: { kind: "answer", text: "Forty-" } }, { event: "error", data: { kind: "error", text: "Cannot reach the model server" } }, endFrame({ interrupted: true }))])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ status: "error", answer: "Forty-", notice: "Cannot reach the model server", interrupted: true });
  });
});

describe("robustness", () => {
  it("ignores frames once the turn has been cleared", () => {
    expect(run([...frames(event("answer", { text: "late" }))])).toBeNull();
    expect(run([start(), { type: "clear" }, ...frames(event("answer", { text: "late" }))])).toBeNull();
  });

  it("ignores events it does not know and data of the wrong shape", () => {
    const turn = run([start(), ...frames({ event: "mystery", data: { a: 1 } }, { event: "answer", data: "not an object" }, { event: "answer", data: null })])!;
    expect(turn).toMatchObject({ answer: "", status: "streaming" });
  });

  it("does not change the turn it was given", () => {
    const before = run([start()])!;
    const snapshot = JSON.stringify(before);
    liveReducer(before, { type: "frame", event: "answer", data: event("answer", { text: "x" }).data });
    expect(JSON.stringify(before)).toBe(snapshot);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `web/tests/workspace/derive.test.ts`**

`deriveThread` lays out the conversation from the saved state and the live turn; `deriveRunPanel` says what the run needs from the person right now (nothing, an answer, or a decision to resume); `shouldKeepLive` says which finished turns stay on screen. None of this is stored: it is worked out from the state each time it is drawn.

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { deriveRunPanel, deriveThread, isWorking, shouldKeepLive, summarizeWork } from "../../src/lib/workspace/derive";
import { liveReducer, type LiveAction, type LiveTurn } from "../../src/lib/workspace/live";
import { endFrame, event, makeRun, makeState } from "./fixtures";

const live = (...actions: LiveAction[]) => actions.reduce<LiveTurn | null>((s, a) => liveReducer(s, a), null);
const frame = (f: { event: string; data: unknown }): LiveAction => ({ type: "frame", event: f.event, data: f.data });
const start: LiveAction = { type: "start", kind: "agent", text: "Collect the plans" };

describe("deriveThread", () => {
  it("is empty for a session with nothing in it", () => {
    expect(deriveThread(makeState(), null)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("lays out saved history as questions and answers, with the work behind each answer", () => {
    const state = makeState({
      history: [
        { role: "user", content: "Collect the plans", reasoning: "", sources: [], interrupted: false, note: "", trace: [] },
        { role: "assistant", content: "Found six plans.", reasoning: "Thinking.", sources: ["https://a.test/"], interrupted: false, note: "6 rows added to the results table", trace: [{ name: "fetch_page", args: {}, text: "ok", ok: true }] },
      ],
    });
    const [user, agent] = deriveThread(state, null);
    expect(user).toMatchObject({ kind: "user", text: "Collect the plans", queued: false });
    expect(agent).toMatchObject({ kind: "agent", answer: "Found six plans.", reasoning: "Thinking.", note: "6 rows added to the results table", sources: ["https://a.test/"], live: false });
    expect(agent.kind === "agent" && agent.trace).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("shows the turn in progress after the history, with what has streamed so far", () => {
    const turn = live(start, frame(event("tool_start", { name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://a.test" } })), frame(event("answer", { text: "Looking" })));
    const items = deriveThread(makeState(), turn);
    expect(items.map((i) => i.kind)).toEqual(["user", "agent"]);
    expect(items[1]).toMatchObject({ kind: "agent", answer: "Looking", live: true });
  });

  it("puts messages the agent has read in the conversation, and the ones it has not read apart as queued", () => {
    const turn = live(start, { type: "queued", id: "f2", text: "skip the blog" }, frame(event("followup", { text: "also the SKU", id: "f1" })));
    const items = deriveThread(makeState(), turn);
    expect(items.filter((i) => i.kind === "user").map((i) => (i.kind === "user" ? [i.text, i.queued] : null))).toEqual([
      ["Collect the plans", false],
      ["also the SKU", false],
      ["skip the blog", true],
    ]);
  });

  it("shows a saved run that is waiting or stopped, with its work log, when nothing is streaming here", () => {
    const run = makeRun({
      status: "paused",
      instruction: "Collect the plans",
      trace: [{ name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://a.test" }, text: "ok", ok: true }],
      followups: [{ id: "f1", text: "also the SKU", delivered: true }, { id: "f2", text: "not read", delivered: false }],
    });
    const items = deriveThread(makeState({ run }), null);
    expect(items.map((i) => (i.kind === "user" ? `user:${i.text}:${i.queued}` : "agent"))).toEqual(["user:Collect the plans:false", "user:also the SKU:false", "agent", "user:not read:true"]);
  });

  it("does not show a finished run twice: it is in the history", () => {
    const state = makeState({
      run: makeRun({ status: "done" }),
      history: [{ role: "user", content: "Collect the plans", reasoning: "", sources: [], interrupted: false, note: "", trace: [] }],
    });
    expect(deriveThread(state, null)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("shows the turn in progress and not also the saved run record for the same run", () => {
    const state = makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "running" }) });
    expect(deriveThread(state, live(start)).filter((i) => i.kind === "user")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("marks a partly streamed answer that was cut off", () => {
    const turn = live({ type: "start", kind: "ask", text: "What does Team cost?" }, frame({ event: "answer", data: { kind: "answer", text: "Forty-" } }), frame({ event: "error", data: { kind: "error", text: "Lost it" } }), frame(endFrame({ interrupted: true })));
    const agent = deriveThread(makeState(), turn).find((i) => i.kind === "agent");
    expect(agent).toMatchObject({ interrupted: true, answer: "Forty-" });
  });
});

describe("deriveRunPanel", () => {
  it("is nothing when there is no run", () => {
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState(), null)).toEqual({ kind: "none" });
  });

  it("describes the call being made while a run streams", () => {
    const turn = live(start, frame(event("step", { step: 3 })), frame(event("tool_start", { name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://shop.example/plans" } })));
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState(), turn)).toEqual({ kind: "working", step: 3, doing: "Fetching shop.example/plans" });
  });

  it("says it is thinking between calls", () => {
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState(), live(start, frame(event("step", { step: 1 }))))).toEqual({ kind: "working", step: 1, doing: "Thinking" });
  });

  it("shows the question when the run is waiting for an answer, live or saved", () => {
    const pending = { kind: "ask" as const, question: "Which?", options: ["A", "B"] };
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState(), live(start, frame(event("needs_input", { payload: pending })), frame(endFrame())))).toEqual({ kind: "question", pending });
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "paused", pending }) }), null)).toEqual({ kind: "question", pending });
  });

  it("offers to resume or discard a stopped or failed run, with the reason", () => {
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "stopped" }) }), null)).toMatchObject({ kind: "interrupted", resumable: true, message: "This run was stopped. Your work so far is saved." });
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "error" }) }), null)).toMatchObject({ kind: "interrupted", resumable: true });
    const failed = live(start, frame(event("error", { text: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day)." })), frame(endFrame()));
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState(), failed)).toEqual({ kind: "interrupted", resumable: true, message: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day)." });
  });

  it("shows nothing in the panel for a run that ended for good: the thread already says why", () => {
    const ended = live(start, frame(event("limit", { text: "context" })), frame(endFrame()));
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState(), ended)).toEqual({ kind: "none" });
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "ended", resumable: false }) }), null)).toEqual({ kind: "none" });
  });

  it("does not offer to resume a run that cannot be resumed", () => {
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "stopped", resumable: false }) }), null)).toMatchObject({ resumable: false });
  });

  it("says it is finishing up when the server still has a run going that this screen is not streaming", () => {
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "running" }), busy: true }), null)).toEqual({ kind: "working", step: 0, doing: "Finishing up" });
  });

  it("is nothing for a finished run", () => {
    expect(deriveRunPanel(makeState({ run: makeRun({ status: "done" }) }), live(start, frame(event("done")), frame(endFrame())))).toEqual({ kind: "none" });
  });
});

describe("isWorking", () => {
  it("is true while streaming here, or while the server says it is busy", () => {
    expect(isWorking(makeState(), live(start))).toBe(true);
    expect(isWorking(makeState({ busy: true }), null)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("is false for a paused or finished turn and an idle server", () => {
    expect(isWorking(makeState(), live(start, frame(event("done")), frame(endFrame())))).toBe(false);
    expect(isWorking(makeState(), null)).toBe(false);
    const paused = live(start, frame(event("needs_input", { payload: { kind: "ask", question: "?", options: [] } })), frame(endFrame()));
    expect(isWorking(makeState(), paused)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("summarizeWork", () => {
  const line = (name: string, ok = true) => ({ name, args: {}, text: "", ok, done: true });
  it("counts the steps, and the pages that were read", () => {
    expect(summarizeWork([line("fetch_page"), line("read_page"), line("fetch_page"), line("add_rows")])).toBe("Worked for 4 steps, read 2 pages");
  });
  it("does not count a page that could not be fetched as read", () => {
    expect(summarizeWork([line("fetch_page"), line("fetch_page", false)])).toBe("Worked for 2 steps, read 1 page");
  });
  it("leaves out the pages when none were read, and says step for one", () => {
    expect(summarizeWork([line("add_rows")])).toBe("Worked for 1 step");
  });
  it("is empty when nothing was done", () => {
    expect(summarizeWork([])).toBe("");
  });
});

describe("shouldKeepLive", () => {
  const finished = (...frames: { event: string; data: unknown }[]) => live(start, ...frames.map(frame), frame(endFrame()))!;
  it("keeps a run that failed, because the saved state does not remember why", () => {
    expect(shouldKeepLive(finished(event("error", { text: "Daily page limit reached (300 a day)." })))).toBe(true);
    expect(shouldKeepLive(finished(event("limit", { text: "context" })))).toBe(true);
  });
  it("lets go of a run that finished, paused or was stopped, because the saved state shows it", () => {
    expect(shouldKeepLive(finished(event("done")))).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldKeepLive(finished(event("needs_input", { payload: { kind: "ask", question: "?", options: [] } })))).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldKeepLive(finished(event("limit", { text: "stopped" })))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("lets go of a question to the pages even when it failed: the answer so far is saved in the history", () => {
    const ask = live({ type: "start", kind: "ask", text: "Q" }, frame({ event: "error", data: { kind: "error", text: "Lost it" } }), frame(endFrame()))!;
    expect(shouldKeepLive(ask)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `web/src/lib/workspace/live.ts`**

```
/** The turn that is streaming into this screen, built from the events of one request. */
import type { EndFrame, Pending, RunView, Usage } from "../api/types";

export type LogEntry = { id: number; name: string; args: Record<string, unknown>; text: string; ok: boolean; done: boolean };
export type QueuedMessage = { id: string; text: string };
export type LiveStatus = "streaming" | "paused" | "done" | "stopped" | "error";

export type LiveTurn = {
  kind: "agent" | "ask";
  text: string;
  status: LiveStatus;
  step: number;
  reasoning: string;
  answer: string;
  log: LogEntry[];
  rowsAdded: number;
  pending: Pending | null;
  notice: string | null;
  /** False once the server says the run ended for good: there is nothing to resume. */
  resumable: boolean;
  followups: string[];
  queued: QueuedMessage[];
  drafts: string[];
  leftovers: string[];
  interrupted: boolean;
  usage: Usage | null;
  ended: boolean;
};

export type LiveAction =
  | { type: "start"; kind: "agent" | "ask"; text: string; from?: RunView }
  | { type: "frame"; event: string; data: unknown }
  | { type: "queued"; id: string; text: string }
  | { type: "failed"; message: string; resumable?: boolean }
  | { type: "resume" }
  | { type: "clear" };

export const LIMIT_MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  context: "The conversation no longer fits the model's context window, so the run ended. Try a narrower instruction.",
};

export const CONNECTION_LOST = "The connection was lost before the run finished. Your work so far is saved.";

function begin(kind: "agent" | "ask", text: string, from?: RunView): LiveTurn {
  const turn: LiveTurn = {
    kind,
    text,
    status: "streaming",
    step: 0,
    reasoning: "",
    answer: "",
    log: [],
    rowsAdded: 0,
    pending: null,
    notice: null,
    resumable: true,
    followups: [],
    queued: [],
    drafts: [],
    leftovers: [],
    interrupted: false,
    usage: null,
    ended: false,
  };
  if (!from) return turn;
  return {
    ...turn,
    rowsAdded: from.rows_added,
    log: from.trace.map((entry, index) => ({ id: index + 1, name: entry.name, args: entry.args, text: entry.text, ok: entry.ok, done: true })),
    followups: from.followups.filter((f) => f.delivered).map((f) => f.text),
    queued: from.followups.filter((f) => !f.delivered).map((f) => ({ id: f.id, text: f.text })),
  };
}

const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> => typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
const str = (value: unknown) => (typeof value === "string" ? value : "");

function applyFrame(turn: LiveTurn, event: string, data: unknown): LiveTurn {
  if (!isRecord(data)) return turn;
  switch (event) {
    case "step":
      return { ...turn, step: Number(data.step) || 0, reasoning: "", answer: "" };
    case "reasoning":
      return { ...turn, reasoning: turn.reasoning + str(data.text) };
    case "answer":
      return { ...turn, answer: turn.answer + str(data.text) };
    case "tool_start": {
      const id = turn.log.reduce((highest, entry) => Math.max(highest, entry.id), 0) + 1;
      const args = isRecord(data.args) ? data.args : {};
      return { ...turn, log: [...turn.log, { id, name: str(data.name), args, text: "", ok: true, done: false }] };
    }
    case "tool_result": {
      const index = turn.log.findIndex((entry) => !entry.done);
      if (index === -1) return turn;
      const log = turn.log.map((entry, i) => (i === index ? { ...entry, text: str(data.text), ok: data.ok !== false, done: true } : entry));
      return { ...turn, log };
    }
    case "rows_added":
      return { ...turn, rowsAdded: turn.rowsAdded + (Number(data.count) || 0) };
    case "needs_input":
      return { ...turn, status: "paused", pending: isRecord(data.payload) ? (data.payload as Pending) : null };
    case "limit": {
      const reason = str(data.text);
      if (reason === "stopped") return { ...turn, status: "stopped", notice: null };
      return { ...turn, status: "error", notice: LIMIT_MESSAGES[reason] ?? reason, resumable: false };
    }
    case "error": {
      const final = isRecord(data.payload) && data.payload.final === true;
      return { ...turn, status: "error", notice: str(data.text), resumable: !final };
    }
    case "done":
      return { ...turn, status: "done" };
    case "followup": {
      const id = str(data.id);
      const text = str(data.text);
      const queued = turn.queued.filter((q) => (id ? q.id !== id : q.text !== text));
      return { ...turn, queued, followups: [...turn.followups, text] };
    }
    case "end": {
      const end = data as Partial<EndFrame>;
      const ended = {
        ...turn,
        ended: true,
        drafts: Array.isArray(end.drafts) ? end.drafts : [],
        leftovers: Array.isArray(end.leftovers) ? end.leftovers : [],
        interrupted: end.interrupted === true,
        usage: end.usage ?? turn.usage,
      };
      if (ended.status !== "streaming") return ended;
      // A question to the pages has no "done" event: finishing the stream is finishing the answer.
      if (turn.kind === "ask") return { ...ended, status: "done" };
      return { ...ended, status: "error", notice: CONNECTION_LOST };
    }
    default:
      return turn;
  }
}

export function liveReducer(state: LiveTurn | null, action: LiveAction): LiveTurn | null {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "start":
      return begin(action.kind, action.text, action.from);
    case "clear":
      return null;
    case "frame":
      return state ? applyFrame(state, action.event, action.data) : null;
    case "queued":
      return state ? { ...state, queued: [...state.queued, { id: action.id, text: action.text }] } : null;
    case "failed":
      return state ? { ...state, status: "error", notice: action.message, resumable: action.resumable ?? false, ended: true } : null;
    case "resume":
      return state ? { ...state, status: "streaming", pending: null, ended: false, notice: null, resumable: true, drafts: [], leftovers: [], interrupted: false } : null;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `web/src/lib/workspace/derive.ts`**

```
/** What the conversation and the run panel show, worked out from the saved state and the turn streaming here. */
import { describeCall } from "../api/format";
import type { Pending, TraceEntry, WorkspaceState } from "../api/types";
import type { LiveTurn } from "./live";

export type TraceLine = TraceEntry & { done: boolean };

export type ThreadItem =
  | { kind: "user"; key: string; text: string; queued: boolean }
  | {
      kind: "agent";
      key: string;
      answer: string;
      reasoning: string;
      note: string;
      sources: string[];
      trace: TraceLine[];
      interrupted: boolean;
      live: boolean;
      notice: string | null;
    };

export type RunPanel =
  | { kind: "none" }
  | { kind: "working"; step: number; doing: string }
  | { kind: "question"; pending: Pending }
  | { kind: "interrupted"; resumable: boolean; message: string };

const rowsNote = (count: number) => (count > 0 ? `${count} ${count === 1 ? "row" : "rows"} added to the results table` : "");
const done = (entry: TraceEntry): TraceLine => ({ ...entry, done: true });

export function deriveThread(state: WorkspaceState, live: LiveTurn | null): ThreadItem[] {
  const items: ThreadItem[] = state.history.map((message, index): ThreadItem =>
    message.role === "user"
      ? { kind: "user", key: `h${index}`, text: message.content, queued: false }
      : {
          kind: "agent",
          key: `h${index}`,
          answer: message.content,
          reasoning: message.reasoning,
          note: message.note,
          sources: message.sources,
          trace: message.trace.map(done),
          interrupted: message.interrupted,
          live: false,
          notice: null,
        },
  );

  if (live) {
    items.push({ kind: "user", key: "live-user", text: live.text, queued: false });
    live.followups.forEach((text, i) => items.push({ kind: "user", key: `live-followup-${i}`, text, queued: false }));
    items.push({
      kind: "agent",
      key: "live-agent",
      answer: live.answer,
      reasoning: live.reasoning,
      note: rowsNote(live.rowsAdded),
      sources: [],
      trace: live.log.map((entry) => ({ name: entry.name, args: entry.args, text: entry.text, ok: entry.ok, done: entry.done })),
      interrupted: live.interrupted,
      live: live.status === "streaming",
      notice: live.status === "error" || live.status === "stopped" ? live.notice : null,
    });
    live.queued.forEach((q) => items.push({ kind: "user", key: `live-queued-${q.id}`, text: q.text, queued: true }));
    return items;
  }

  const run = state.run;
  if (run && run.status !== "done" && run.status !== "ended") {
    items.push({ kind: "user", key: "run-user", text: run.instruction, queued: false });
    run.followups.filter((f) => f.delivered).forEach((f) => items.push({ kind: "user", key: `run-followup-${f.id}`, text: f.text, queued: false }));
    items.push({
      kind: "agent",
      key: "run-agent",
      answer: "",
      reasoning: "",
      note: rowsNote(run.rows_added),
      sources: [],
      trace: run.trace.map(done),
      interrupted: false,
      live: false,
      notice: null,
    });
    run.followups.filter((f) => !f.delivered).forEach((f) => items.push({ kind: "user", key: `run-queued-${f.id}`, text: f.text, queued: true }));
  }
  return items;
}

const STOPPED = "This run was stopped. Your work so far is saved.";

export function deriveRunPanel(state: WorkspaceState, live: LiveTurn | null): RunPanel {
  if (live) {
    if (live.kind === "ask") return { kind: "none" };
    switch (live.status) {
      case "streaming": {
        const calling = [...live.log].reverse().find((entry) => !entry.done);
        return { kind: "working", step: live.step, doing: calling ? describeCall(calling.name, calling.args) : "Thinking" };
      }
      case "paused":
        return live.pending ? { kind: "question", pending: live.pending } : { kind: "interrupted", resumable: true, message: STOPPED };
      case "stopped":
        return { kind: "interrupted", resumable: true, message: STOPPED };
      case "error":
        return live.resumable ? { kind: "interrupted", resumable: true, message: live.notice ?? STOPPED } : { kind: "none" };
      default:
        return { kind: "none" };
    }
  }

  const run = state.run;
  if (!run) return { kind: "none" };
  switch (run.status) {
    case "running":
      return state.busy
        ? { kind: "working", step: 0, doing: "Finishing up" }
        : { kind: "interrupted", resumable: run.resumable, message: "This run was interrupted. Your work so far is saved." };
    case "paused":
      return run.pending ? { kind: "question", pending: run.pending } : { kind: "interrupted", resumable: run.resumable, message: STOPPED };
    case "stopped":
      return { kind: "interrupted", resumable: run.resumable, message: STOPPED };
    case "error":
      return { kind: "interrupted", resumable: run.resumable, message: "This run stopped because of an error. Your work so far is saved." };
    default:
      return { kind: "none" };
  }
}

export function isWorking(state: WorkspaceState, live: LiveTurn | null): boolean {
  return live ? live.status === "streaming" : state.busy;
}

const plural = (count: number, word: string) => `${count} ${word}${count === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

/** "Worked for 7 steps, read 3 pages": the line above a collapsed work log. */
export function summarizeWork(trace: TraceLine[]): string {
  if (trace.length === 0) return "";
  const read = trace.filter((line) => line.name === "fetch_page" && line.ok).length;
  return `Worked for ${plural(trace.length, "step")}${read > 0 ? `, read ${plural(read, "page")}` : ""}`;
}

/** Whether the turn that just finished streaming stays on screen. Most are replaced by the saved state; a failure is kept, because the saved state does not say why it failed. */
export function shouldKeepLive(turn: LiveTurn): boolean {
  return turn.kind === "agent" && turn.status === "error";
}
```

### Task 6: Keep a results table's columns in the agent's order

A small fix to plan 1's storage, made first because the results table and the CSV download in the next task depend on it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/scraper_api/db.py`, `src/scraper_api/pgstore.py`, `tests/test_pgstore.py`, `tests/test_db.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PgSessionStore.append_rows` and `migrate` from plan 1.
- Produces: `app.rows.data` is `json`, not `jsonb`; `migrate` converts a database made earlier.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add a test to `tests/test_pgstore.py`**

Found while building the results table in the next task: Postgres `jsonb` sorts an object's keys (shortest first), so a table loaded from the database came back with different columns order from the one the agent chose. Row `{price, plan, page}` returned as `{page, plan, price}`.

```python
def test_rows_keep_the_order_of_their_columns_after_a_reload(pg):
    # Postgres jsonb sorts keys (shortest first), which would scramble the table's columns once a session is loaded again.
    PgSessionStore(pg, "alice", CFG).append_rows([{"price": "$9", "plan": "Solo", "page": "shop.example/plans"}])
    reloaded = PgSessionStore(pg, "alice", CFG).load()
    assert list(reloaded.rows[0]) == ["price", "plan", "page"]
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Add a test to `tests/test_db.py`**

```python
def test_migrate_turns_an_older_jsonb_rows_table_into_one_that_keeps_column_order(pg_pool):
    with pg_pool.connection() as conn:
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE app.rows ALTER COLUMN data TYPE jsonb")
    migrate(pg_pool)
    with pg_pool.connection() as conn:
        kind = conn.execute("SELECT data_type FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = 'app' AND table_name = 'rows' AND column_name = 'data'").fetchone()[0]
    assert kind == "json"
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `web/tests/workspace/ledger.test.ts`**

The results table has whatever columns the agent used, in the order they first appear. Cells that are numbers or prices line up on the right. A list or an object in a cell is written as JSON, never as `[object Object]`.

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { cellText, ledgerColumns, looksNumeric, meterShare, resetNote } from "../../src/lib/workspace/ledger";
import { usage } from "./fixtures";

describe("ledgerColumns", () => {
  it("is every key the rows use, in the order they first appear", () => {
    expect(ledgerColumns([{ plan: "Solo", price: "$9" }, { plan: "Team", seats: 5, price: "$49" }])).toEqual(["plan", "price", "seats"]);
  });
  it("is empty when there are no rows", () => {
    expect(ledgerColumns([])).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("cellText", () => {
  it("shows text and numbers as they are, and nothing for a missing value", () => {
    expect(cellText("Solo")).toBe("Solo");
    expect(cellText(9)).toBe("9");
    expect(cellText(true)).toBe("true");
    expect(cellText(null)).toBe("");
    expect(cellText(undefined)).toBe("");
  });
  it("writes lists and objects out as JSON, so nothing renders as [object Object]", () => {
    expect(cellText({ a: 1 })).toBe('{"a":1}');
    expect(cellText(["x", "y"])).toBe('["x","y"]');
  });
});

describe("meterShare", () => {
  it("is the used part as a percentage, never more than 100 and never less than 0", () => {
    expect(meterShare(118, 300)).toBe(39);
    expect(meterShare(400, 300)).toBe(100);
    expect(meterShare(-3, 300)).toBe(0);
  });
  it("is zero when there is no limit to measure against", () => {
    expect(meterShare(5, 0)).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("resetNote", () => {
  it("names the time the allowance starts again, in the viewer's own clock", () => {
    const text = resetNote(usage, "en-US", "UTC");
    expect(text).toMatch(/^Starts again /);
    expect(text).toContain("12:00");
  });
  it("is plain when the time cannot be read", () => {
    expect(resetNote({ ...usage, resets_at: "nonsense" }, "en-US", "UTC")).toBe("Starts again at midnight UTC.");
  });
});

describe("looksNumeric", () => {
  it("is true for numbers, prices and percentages, which line up on the right", () => {
    for (const text of ["9", "1,250", "12.5", "$49", "€1,299.00", "-3", "40%"]) expect(looksNumeric(text), text).toBe(true);
  });
  it("is false for words, dates, codes and empty cells", () => {
    for (const text of ["", "Solo", "2026-10-10", "A-100", "$49 a month", "1.2.3"]) expect(looksNumeric(text), text).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `web/src/lib/workspace/ledger.ts`**

```
/** Small helpers for the results table and the allowance meter. */
import type { Usage } from "../api/types";

/** Every key the rows use, in the order they first appear. */
export function ledgerColumns(rows: Record<string, unknown>[]): string[] {
  const columns = new Set<string>();
  for (const row of rows) for (const key of Object.keys(row)) columns.add(key);
  return [...columns];
}

export function cellText(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
  if (typeof value === "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  return String(value);
}

/** The used part of an allowance as a whole percentage from 0 to 100. */
export function meterShare(used: number, limit: number): number {
  if (!(limit > 0)) return 0;
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round((used / limit) * 100)));
}

export function resetNote(usage: Usage, locale?: string, timeZone?: string): string {
  const at = new Date(usage.resets_at);
  if (Number.isNaN(at.getTime())) return "Starts again at midnight UTC.";
  const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", timeZone, timeZoneName: "short" }).format(at);
  return `Starts again at ${time}.`;
}

const NUMERIC = /^[-+]?[$€£¥]?\d[\d,]*(\.\d+)?%?$/;

/** Cells like `$49`, `1,250` and `40%` line up on the right, so a column of figures reads down. */
export function looksNumeric(text: string): boolean {
  return NUMERIC.test(text);
}
```
