- [ ] **Step 5: Create `web/e2e/support/seed.ts`**

The server will not fetch addresses on this machine (that is the point of its guard), so a browser test cannot scrape a local page. This writes a saved session straight into the `app` schema instead, in the shape the API saves, so a test can start from a conversation with results.

```
/**
 * Giving a person something to look at without running the agent. The server will not fetch addresses on this machine (that is the
 * point of its guard), so a browser test cannot scrape a local page. It writes the saved session straight into the `app` schema instead,
 * in the same shape the Python API saves.
 */
import pg from "pg";
import { env } from "./env";

export type SeedPage = { url: string; title: string; markdown?: string };
export type SeedMessage = { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string; note?: string; trace?: { name: string; args: Record<string, unknown>; text: string; ok: boolean }[] };

/** The id Better Auth gave this person, read from their session cookie. */
export async function userIdOf(cookie: string): Promise<string> {
  const response = await fetch(`${env.web}/api/auth/get-session`, { headers: { cookie } });
  const body = (await response.json()) as { user?: { id?: string } } | null;
  if (!body?.user?.id) throw new Error("that cookie is not a signed-in session");
  return body.user.id;
}

export async function seedSession(userId: string, seed: { pages?: SeedPage[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; history?: SeedMessage[] }): Promise<void> {
  const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: env.database });
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.query("BEGIN");
    for (const table of ["pages", "rows", "history"]) await client.query(`DELETE FROM app.${table} WHERE user_id = $1`, [userId]);
    await client.query("INSERT INTO app.sessions (user_id, session_id, run_counter) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO NOTHING", [userId, `seed-${Date.now()}`, 0]);
    for (const [position, page] of (seed.pages ?? []).entries()) {
      const markdown = page.markdown ?? `# ${page.title}\n\nSeeded page text for ${page.url}.`;
      await client.query(
        "INSERT INTO app.pages (user_id, key, position, url, title, markdown, links, html, looks_empty) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, '[]'::jsonb, '', false)",
        [userId, page.url, position, page.url, page.title, markdown],
      );
    }
    for (const row of seed.rows ?? []) await client.query("INSERT INTO app.rows (user_id, data) VALUES ($1, $2::json)", [userId, JSON.stringify(row)]);
    for (const message of seed.history ?? []) {
      await client.query(
        "INSERT INTO app.history (user_id, role, content, reasoning, sources, interrupted, note, trace) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, '', '[]'::jsonb, false, $4, $5::jsonb)",
        [userId, message.role, message.content, message.note ?? "", JSON.stringify(message.trace ?? [])],
      );
    }
    await client.query("COMMIT");
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("ROLLBACK");
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await client.end();
  }
}

export const PLAN_ROWS = [
  { plan: "Solo", price: "$9", page: "shop.example/plans" },
  { plan: "Team", price: "$49", page: "shop.example/plans/team" },
  { plan: "Studio", price: "$99", page: "shop.example/plans/studio" },
];

export const PLAN_HISTORY: SeedMessage[] = [
  { role: "user", content: "Go to shop.example/plans and collect every plan with its price." },
  {
    role: "assistant",
    content: "I found three plans on three pages and added them to the table.\n\nTeam and Studio each show two prices.",
    note: "3 rows added to the results table",
    trace: [
      { name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://shop.example/plans" }, text: "Fetched", ok: true },
      { name: "add_rows", args: { rows: [{}, {}, {}] }, text: "Added 3 rows", ok: true },
    ],
  },
];

export const PLAN_PAGES: SeedPage[] = [
  { url: "https://shop.example/plans", title: "Plans and pricing" },
  { url: "https://shop.example/plans/team", title: "Team plan" },
];

/** Today's count of model calls, so a person can be put at their daily limit. */
export async function seedUsage(userId: string, modelCalls: number): Promise<void> {
  const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: env.database });
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.query(
      "INSERT INTO app.usage (user_id, day, model_calls, pages) VALUES ($1, (now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date, $2, 0) ON CONFLICT (user_id, day) DO UPDATE SET model_calls = EXCLUDED.model_calls",
      [userId, modelCalls],
    );
  } finally {
    await client.end();
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `web/e2e/specs/workspace.spec.ts`**

```
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { createMember, ownerCookie, signInBrowser } from "../support/journeys";
import { PLAN_HISTORY, PLAN_PAGES, PLAN_ROWS, seedSession, userIdOf } from "../support/seed";

test.describe("the workspace", () => {
  test("a person with nothing yet is invited to start, and the results say what will appear", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("empty");
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "What should it collect?" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("Rows the agent collects appear here")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Download CSV" })).toBeDisabled();
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "New session" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("shows the saved conversation, the results table and the pages read", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("saved");
    await seedSession(await userIdOf(member.cookie), { pages: PLAN_PAGES, rows: PLAN_ROWS, history: PLAN_HISTORY });
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/");

    await expect(page.getByText("Go to shop.example/plans and collect every plan with its price.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("I found three plans on three pages")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("3 rows added to the results table")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("Worked for 2 steps, read 1 page")).toBeVisible();

    const table = page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" });
    await expect(table.getByRole("columnheader", { name: "plan" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(table.getByRole("row")).toHaveCount(4);
    await expect(table.getByRole("cell", { name: "Studio", exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(table.getByRole("cell", { name: "$49", exact: true })).toHaveClass(/num/);
    await expect(page.getByText("3 rows", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Pages read" })).toBeVisible();
    const pages = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Pages read" });
    await expect(pages.getByText("Plans and pricing")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(pages.getByText("shop.example/plans/team")).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("shows an answer that quotes a web page as formatted text only", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("hostile");
    const hostile = "Heading text ![tracker](http://evil.test/pixel.png) <img src=x onerror=alert(1)> [bad](javascript:alert(1)) [good](https://example.org/page).\n\n<script>window.hacked = true</script>";
    await seedSession(await userIdOf(member.cookie), { history: [{ role: "user", content: "Say hello." }, { role: "assistant", content: hostile }] });
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    const requested: string[] = [];
    page.on("request", (request) => requested.push(request.url()));
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.locator(".prose a", { hasText: "good" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "https://example.org/page");
    await expect(page.locator(".prose a", { hasText: "good" })).toHaveAttribute("rel", /noopener/);
    await expect(page.locator(".prose img")).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.locator(".prose a[href^='javascript']")).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.locator(".prose script")).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { hacked?: boolean }).hacked)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(requested.some((url) => url.includes("evil.test"))).toBe(false);
  });

  test("downloads the results as CSV and as JSON", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("download");
    await seedSession(await userIdOf(member.cookie), { rows: PLAN_ROWS });
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/");

    const csv = page.waitForEvent("download");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Download CSV" }).click();
    const csvFile = await csv;
    expect(csvFile.suggestedFilename()).toBe("results.csv");
    const csvText = await (await import("node:fs/promises")).readFile((await csvFile.path())!, "utf8");
    expect(csvText.split("\n")[0]).toBe("plan,price,page");
    expect(csvText).toContain("Studio,$99,shop.example/plans/studio");

    const json = page.waitForEvent("download");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Download JSON" }).click();
    const jsonFile = await json;
    expect(jsonFile.suggestedFilename()).toBe("results.json");
    const parsed = JSON.parse(await (await import("node:fs/promises")).readFile((await jsonFile.path())!, "utf8"));
    expect(parsed).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  test("the account menu shows who is signed in and today's allowance, and offers invitations only to an administrator", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("menu");
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Account menu" }).click();
    const allowance = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Today's allowance" });
    await expect(allowance.getByText("Pages read today")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(allowance.getByText("0 of 300").first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText(member.email)).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "Invitations and people" })).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
    await expect(allowance).toHaveCount(0);

    await context.clearCookies();
    await signInBrowser(context, await ownerCookie());
    await page.goto("/");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Account menu" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "Invitations and people" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("starts a new session only after asking, and then shows the empty workspace", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("fresh");
    await seedSession(await userIdOf(member.cookie), { pages: PLAN_PAGES, rows: PLAN_ROWS, history: PLAN_HISTORY });
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" })).toBeVisible();

    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "New session" }).click();
    const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Start a new session?" });
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Keep working" }).click();
    await expect(dialog).toBeHidden();
    await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" })).toBeVisible();

    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "New session" }).click();
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Clear everything" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "What should it collect?" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" })).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByText("Rows the agent collects appear here")).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("on a phone, chat and results are two tabs", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("phone");
    await seedSession(await userIdOf(member.cookie), { rows: PLAN_ROWS, history: PLAN_HISTORY });
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 800 });
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByText("I found three plans on three pages")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" })).toBeHidden();
    await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Results (3)" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("I found three plans on three pages")).toBeHidden();
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("a signed-out visitor is sent to sign in", async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `web/src/lib/workspace/state.ts`**

The server's state for this person (`GET /state`) is a TanStack Query. After anything changes it, ask for it again with `useRefreshState`.

```
"use client";

import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useCallback } from "react";
import { useApi } from "../../components/workspace/ApiProvider";
import type { WorkspaceState } from "../api/types";

export const STATE_KEY = ["state"] as const;

export function useWorkspaceState() {
  const api = useApi();
  return useQuery({ queryKey: STATE_KEY, queryFn: () => api.get<WorkspaceState>("/state") });
}

/** Ask the server for the state again, and resolve when it has arrived. */
export function useRefreshState() {
  const queries = useQueryClient();
  return useCallback(() => queries.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: STATE_KEY }), [queries]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Create `web/src/components/Dialog.tsx`**

A modal on the browser's own `<dialog>`: focus is trapped, Escape closes it, and focus returns to where it was.

```
"use client";

import { useEffect, useId, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";

/** A modal built on the browser's own <dialog>: it traps focus, closes on Escape and puts focus back where it was. */
export function Dialog({ open, title, onClose, children, actions }: { open: boolean; title: string; onClose: () => void; children?: ReactNode; actions: ReactNode }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = ref.current;
    if (!dialog) return;
    if (open && !dialog.open) dialog.showModal();
    if (!open && dialog.open) dialog.close();
  }, [open]);
  return (
    <dialog ref={ref} aria-labelledby={titleId} onClose={onClose}>
      <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
      {children}
      <div className="actions">{actions}</div>
    </dialog>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Create `web/src/components/workspace/ApiProvider.tsx`**

Gives every part of the workspace the API client and the query cache. The API's address is a prop from the server page, so no build-time setting is needed.

```
"use client";

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { authClient } from "../../lib/auth-client";
import { ApiError, createApiClient, type ApiClient } from "../../lib/api/client";
import { createTokenCache } from "../../lib/api/token";

const ApiContext = createContext<ApiClient | null>(null);

async function fetchToken(): Promise<string> {
  const { data, error } = await authClient.token();
  if (error || !data?.token) throw new ApiError(401, "unauthorized", "Your session has ended. Sign in again.");
  return data.token;
}

/** Gives every part of the workspace the API client and the query cache. `apiUrl` comes from the server, so no build-time setting is needed. */
export function WorkspaceProviders({ apiUrl, children }: { apiUrl: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [api] = useState(() => createApiClient({ baseUrl: apiUrl, tokens: createTokenCache({ fetchToken }) }));
  const [queries] = useState(() => new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, refetchOnWindowFocus: true, staleTime: 0 } } }));
  return (
    <ApiContext.Provider value={api}>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queries}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
    </ApiContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useApi(): ApiClient {
  const api = useContext(ApiContext);
  if (!api) throw new Error("useApi needs WorkspaceProviders around it");
  return api;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Create `web/src/components/workspace/AccountMenu.tsx`**

Who is signed in, today's allowance (pages read and questions to the model, with when it starts again), a link to the administrator page for administrators, sign out, and (once Task 10 adds it) deleting the account.

```
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Usage } from "../../lib/api/types";
import { meterShare, resetNote } from "../../lib/workspace/ledger";
import { SignOutButton } from "../SignOutButton";

export type Person = { name: string; email: string; isAdmin: boolean };

function Meter({ label, used, limit }: { label: string; used: number; limit: number }) {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="row">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <span>
          {used} of {limit}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="bar" role="presentation">
        <i style={{ width: `${meterShare(used, limit)}%` }} />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function AccountMenu({ person, usage, onDelete }: { person: Person; usage: Usage | null; onDelete?: () => void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const root = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const panelId = useId();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const close = (event: Event) => {
      if (event instanceof KeyboardEvent ? event.key === "Escape" : !root.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", close);
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", close);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", close);
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", close);
    };
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="menu" ref={root}>
      <button className="btn" type="button" aria-label="Account menu" aria-expanded={open} aria-controls={panelId} onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)}>
        {person.name.split(" ")[0] || person.email}
      </button>
      {open ? (
        <div className="menu-panel" id={panelId}>
          <div className="who">
            <b>{person.name}</b>
            <span>{person.email}</span>
          </div>
          {usage ? (
            <div className="meter" role="group" aria-label="Today's allowance">
              <Meter label="Pages read today" used={usage.pages} limit={usage.pages_limit} />
              <Meter label="Questions to the model today" used={usage.model_calls} limit={usage.model_calls_limit} />
              <span className="muted">{resetNote(usage)}</span>
            </div>
          ) : null}
          <hr />
          {person.isAdmin ? (
            <Link className="item" href="/admin">
              Invitations and people
            </Link>
          ) : null}
          <SignOutButton className="item" />
          {onDelete ? (
            <button className="item btn-danger" type="button" onClick={() => { setOpen(false); onDelete(); }}>
              Delete my account
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Ledger.tsx`**

```
import { cellText, ledgerColumns, looksNumeric } from "../../lib/workspace/ledger";

/** The results table. Columns are whatever keys the agent used, so a new key adds a column. */
export function Ledger({ rows }: { rows: Record<string, unknown>[] }) {
  const columns = ledgerColumns(rows);
  return (
    <div className="ledger-scroll">
      <table className="ledger">
        <caption className="sr">Collected results</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th className="n" scope="col">
              #
            </th>
            {columns.map((column) => (
              <th key={column} scope="col">
                {column}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row, index) => (
            <tr key={index}>
              <td className="n">{index + 1}</td>
              {columns.map((column) => {
                const text = cellText(row[column]);
                return (
                  <td key={column} className={looksNumeric(text) ? "num" : undefined}>
                    {text}
                  </td>
                );
              })}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Create `web/src/components/workspace/PagesList.tsx`**

Highlighted pages are the ones the model used for its last answer.

```
import { hostAndPath } from "../../lib/api/format";
import type { WorkspaceState } from "../../lib/api/types";

export function PagesList({ pages, failed }: Pick<WorkspaceState, "pages" | "failed">) {
  if (pages.length === 0 && failed.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section className="pages" aria-labelledby="pages-heading">
      <h3 id="pages-heading">Pages read</h3>
      <ul>
        {pages.map((page) => (
          <li key={page.url} className={page.used ? "used" : undefined}>
            <span className="t">{page.title || hostAndPath(page.url)}</span>
            <span className="meta">{page.looks_empty ? "Looks empty" : `${page.chars.toLocaleString("en-US")} characters`}</span>
            <span className="u">{hostAndPath(page.url)}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
        {failed.map((miss) => (
          <li key={miss.url} className="failed">
            <span className="t">Could not read</span>
            <span className="u">{hostAndPath(miss.url)}</span>
            <span className="meta">{miss.reason}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {pages.some((page) => page.used) ? <p className="muted small">Highlighted pages were used for the last answer.</p> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Markdown.tsx`**

The agent's answer can quote any web page, so it is shown as formatted text and nothing more: no raw HTML, no images (a picture's address could carry what the page said to someone else's server), and only ordinary links, opened in a new tab.

```
"use client";

import ReactMarkdown from "react-markdown";
import remarkGfm from "remark-gfm";

const SAFE_LINK = /^(https?:|mailto:)/i;

/**
 * The agent's answer can quote text from any web page, so it is shown as formatted text and nothing more: no raw HTML, no images
 * (a picture's address could carry what the page said to someone else's server), and only ordinary links, opened in a new tab.
 */
export default function Markdown({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return (
    <ReactMarkdown
      remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
      skipHtml
      disallowedElements={["img"]}
      components={{
        a: ({ href, children }) =>
          href && SAFE_LINK.test(href) ? (
            <a href={href} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
              {children}
            </a>
          ) : (
            <>{children}</>
          ),
      }}
    >
      {text}
    </ReactMarkdown>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 17: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Prose.tsx`**

The formatting library is the largest piece of the page, and it is only needed once there is an answer, so it loads on demand. Until it has loaded, the answer is shown as plain paragraphs.

```
"use client";

import { lazy, Suspense } from "react";

// The formatting library is the largest piece of the page and an answer only needs it once there is one, so it loads on demand.
const Markdown = lazy(() => import("./Markdown"));

function PlainText({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      {text
        .split(/\n{2,}/)
        .filter((part) => part.trim())
        .map((part, index) => (
          <p key={index}>{part}</p>
        ))}
    </>
  );
}

export function Prose({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="prose">
      <Suspense fallback={<PlainText text={text} />}>
        <Markdown text={text} />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 18: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Thread.tsx`**

```
import { describeCall, hostAndPath } from "../../lib/api/format";
import { summarizeWork, type ThreadItem, type TraceLine } from "../../lib/workspace/derive";
import { Prose } from "./Prose";

export function WorkLog({ trace, open }: { trace: TraceLine[]; open?: boolean }) {
  if (trace.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <details className="worklog" open={open}>
      <summary>{summarizeWork(trace)}</summary>
      <ul>
        {trace.map((line, index) => (
          <li key={index} className={line.ok ? undefined : "failed"}>
            {line.done ? describeCall(line.name, line.args) : `${describeCall(line.name, line.args)} …`}
            {line.ok ? null : <span className="why"> ({line.text || "it failed"})</span>}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </details>
  );
}

export function EmptyThread({ examples, onPick }: { examples: string[]; onPick?: (text: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="empty">
      <h2>What should it collect?</h2>
      <p>Describe the data you want and where to find it. It reads the pages, asks you when something is unclear, and puts what it finds in a table you can download.</p>
      {onPick ? (
        <div className="examples">
          {examples.map((example) => (
            <button key={example} type="button" onClick={() => onPick(example)}>
              {example}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

const isWebAddress = (url: string) => /^https?:\/\//i.test(url);

export function Thread({ items, onRetry }: { items: ThreadItem[]; onRetry?: () => void }) {
  const last = items.length - 1;
  return (
    <>
      {items.map((item, index) =>
        item.kind === "user" ? (
          <div key={item.key} className={item.queued ? "msg-user queued" : "msg-user"}>
            {item.text}
            {item.queued ? <span className="note">Waiting for the agent to read this</span> : null}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div key={item.key} className="msg-agent">
            <WorkLog trace={item.trace} open={item.live} />
            {item.reasoning ? (
              <details className="reasoning" open={item.live && !item.answer}>
                <summary>Reasoning</summary>
                <div>{item.reasoning}</div>
              </details>
            ) : null}
            {item.answer ? <Prose text={item.answer} /> : item.live && !item.reasoning && item.trace.length === 0 ? <p className="thinking">Thinking…</p> : null}
            {item.sources.length ? (
              <ul className="sources" aria-label="Pages this answer used">
                {item.sources.filter(isWebAddress).map((source) => (
                  <li key={source}>
                    <a href={source} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                      {hostAndPath(source)}
                    </a>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            ) : null}
            {item.note ? <p className="note-line">{item.note}</p> : null}
            {item.interrupted ? (
              <p className="note-line">
                This answer was cut off.{" "}
                {index === last && onRetry ? (
                  <button className="link" type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
                    Ask again
                  </button>
                ) : null}
              </p>
            ) : null}
            {item.notice ? (
              <p className="notice" role="alert">
                {item.notice}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ),
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 19: Create `web/src/components/workspace/workspace.css`**

The layout of the mock: a conversation on the left and the results ledger on the right, tabs on a narrow screen.

```
/* The workspace: a conversation on the left and the results ledger on the right. The ledger is the one memorable object;
   the highlighter yellow appears only where it means "the model used this". */
.workspace { height: 100%; }
.work-status { flex: 1; display: grid; place-content: center; justify-items: center; gap: 0.8rem; padding: 2rem; color: var(--muted); }
/* Top bar */
.menu { position: relative; }
.menu-panel {
  position: absolute; right: 0; top: calc(100% + 0.4rem); z-index: 20; width: min(20rem, 88vw); padding: 0.75rem;
  background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; box-shadow: var(--shadow); display: grid; gap: 0.6rem;
}
.menu-panel .who { display: grid; }
.menu-panel .who span { color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--step--1); }
.meter { display: grid; gap: 0.25rem; font-size: var(--step--1); }
.meter div.row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; }
.meter .bar { height: 5px; border-radius: 3px; background: var(--sunken); overflow: hidden; }
.meter .bar i { display: block; height: 100%; background: var(--accent); }
.menu-panel .item { text-align: left; background: transparent; border: 0; padding: 0.45rem 0.5rem; border-radius: 6px; }
.menu-panel .item:hover { background: var(--sunken); }
.menu-panel hr { border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--rule); margin: 0; width: 100%; }

/* Workspace */
.work { flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.05fr) minmax(0, 1fr); }
.pane { min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.pane + .pane { border-left: 1px solid var(--rule); background: var(--surface); }
.tabs { display: none; }

.thread { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; padding: 1.5rem 1.25rem 1rem; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 1.25rem; }
.thread > * { width: min(100%, 44rem); margin-inline: auto; }
.msg-user {
  align-self: flex-end; background: var(--sunken); border-radius: 12px 12px 4px 12px; padding: 0.7rem 0.95rem; max-width: 92%;
}
.msg-user.queued { background: transparent; border: 1.5px dashed var(--muted); }
.msg-user .note { display: block; margin-top: 0.3rem; font-size: var(--step--1); color: var(--muted); }
.msg-agent { display: grid; gap: 0.7rem; }
.msg-agent .prose { font-family: var(--font-read); font-size: var(--step-1); line-height: 1.65; max-width: 65ch; }
.msg-agent .prose p + p { margin-top: 0.7em; }
.worklog { border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 8px; background: var(--surface); }
.worklog summary { list-style: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 0.5rem 0.8rem; color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--step--1); display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center; }
.worklog summary::-webkit-details-marker { display: none; }
.worklog summary::before { content: ""; width: 0.45rem; height: 0.45rem; border-right: 2px solid currentColor; border-bottom: 2px solid currentColor; transform: rotate(-45deg); transition: transform 120ms ease; }
.worklog[open] summary::before { transform: rotate(45deg); }
.worklog ul { margin: 0; padding: 0.2rem 0.8rem 0.7rem 2rem; font-size: var(--step--1); color: var(--muted); display: grid; gap: 0.25rem; }
.worklog li b { color: var(--ink); font-weight: 560; }

/* Results ledger */
.ledger-head { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.6rem; padding: 1rem 1.25rem 0.6rem; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.ledger-head h2 { font-size: var(--step-2); font-family: var(--font-read); font-weight: 600; margin-right: auto; }
.ledger-head .count { color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--step--1); }
.ledger-scroll { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; padding: 0 1.25rem 1rem; }
table.ledger { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: var(--step-0); }
.ledger th { position: sticky; top: 0; background: var(--surface); text-align: left; font-weight: 650; font-size: var(--step--1); color: var(--muted); padding: 0.45rem 0.6rem; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--ink); white-space: nowrap; }
.ledger td { padding: 0.5rem 0.6rem; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule); vertical-align: top; }
.ledger .n { color: var(--muted); width: 2.2rem; text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.ledger .num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.ledger td.src { color: var(--muted); max-width: 12rem; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.ledger tr.fresh td { animation: settle 2.6s ease-out; }
@keyframes settle { from { background: var(--mark-soft); } to { background: transparent; } }

.pages { border-top: 1px solid var(--rule); padding: 0.8rem 1.25rem 1rem; display: grid; gap: 0.5rem; }
.pages h3 { font-size: var(--step-0); font-weight: 650; }
.pages ul { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: grid; gap: 0.4rem; }
.pages li { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr auto; gap: 0.1rem 0.8rem; align-items: baseline; font-size: var(--step--1); }
.pages .t { font-weight: 560; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.pages li.used .t { background: linear-gradient(transparent 58%, var(--mark) 58%); width: fit-content; padding-inline: 0.1rem; }
.pages .u { color: var(--muted); grid-column: 1; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.pages .meta { color: var(--muted); text-align: right; }

.empty { margin: auto; max-width: 30rem; display: grid; gap: 0.9rem; padding: 2rem 1.25rem; }
.empty h2 { font-family: var(--font-read); font-size: var(--step-3); font-weight: 600; line-height: 1.2; }
.empty p { color: var(--muted); }
.examples { display: grid; gap: 0.5rem; }
.examples button { text-align: left; padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem; border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 8px; background: var(--surface); font-family: var(--font-read); }
.examples button:hover { border-color: var(--accent); }
.ledger-empty { padding: 2rem 1.25rem; color: var(--muted); max-width: 26rem; }


@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .work { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .tabs { display: flex; gap: 0.25rem; padding: 0.4rem 1rem 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule); }
  .tabs button { border: 0; background: transparent; padding: 0.5rem 0.8rem; border-bottom: 2px solid transparent; color: var(--muted); font-weight: 600; }
  .tabs button[aria-selected="true"] { color: var(--ink); border-bottom-color: var(--accent); }
  .pane + .pane { border-left: 0; }
  .work[data-tab="chat"] .pane.results, .work[data-tab="results"] .pane.chat { display: none; }
  .thread { padding-inline: 1rem; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .worklog summary::before { transition: none; }
}
.msg-agent .note-line { color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--step--1); }
.msg-agent .notice { padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem; border-radius: 8px; background: var(--bad-soft); }
.worklog li.failed .why { color: var(--bad); }
.pages li.failed .t { color: var(--bad); }
.menu-panel a.item { display: block; color: inherit; text-decoration: none; }
.menu-panel .item { width: 100%; }
.work-status p { margin: 0; }
.msg-agent .reasoning { color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--step--1); }
.msg-agent .reasoning summary { cursor: pointer; }
.msg-agent .reasoning div { white-space: pre-wrap; padding: 0.4rem 0 0 0.9rem; }
.msg-agent .thinking { color: var(--muted); font-style: italic; }
.msg-agent .sources { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.3rem 0.9rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; font-size: var(--step--1); }
.msg-agent .prose a { overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.msg-agent .prose ul, .msg-agent .prose ol { margin: 0.5em 0; padding-left: 1.4em; }
.msg-agent .prose table { border-collapse: collapse; font-size: var(--step-0); display: block; overflow-x: auto; }
.msg-agent .prose th, .msg-agent .prose td { border: 1px solid var(--rule); padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem; }
.msg-agent .prose pre { overflow-x: auto; background: var(--sunken); padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem; border-radius: 8px; font-size: var(--step--1); }
.msg-agent .prose code { font-size: 0.9em; }
```

- [ ] **Step 20: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Workspace.tsx`**

The conversation here is read-only: it shows what is saved. The composer and the live run arrive in Task 8.

```
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { ApiError } from "../../lib/api/client";
import { deriveThread } from "../../lib/workspace/derive";
import { useWorkspaceState, useRefreshState } from "../../lib/workspace/state";
import type { Person } from "./AccountMenu";
import { useApi } from "./ApiProvider";
import { Dialog } from "../Dialog";
import { ResultsPane } from "./ResultsPane";
import { EmptyThread, Thread } from "./Thread";
import { TopBar } from "./TopBar";
import "./workspace.css";

export function Workspace({ person }: { person: Person }) {
  const api = useApi();
  const router = useRouter();
  const state = useWorkspaceState();
  const refresh = useRefreshState();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"chat" | "results">("chat");
  const [confirmNew, setConfirmNew] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.error instanceof ApiError && state.error.status === 401) router.replace("/sign-in");
  }, [state.error, router]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3200);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  const data = state.data;
  const thread = useMemo(() => (data ? deriveThread(data, null) : []), [data]);

  if (!data) {
    return (
      <div className="page">
        <TopBar person={person} usage={null} canReset={false} onNewSession={() => {}} />
        <div className="work-status" role={state.error ? "alert" : "status"}>
          {state.error ? (
            <>
              <p>{state.error instanceof ApiError ? state.error.message : "Could not load your workspace."}</p>
              <button className="btn" type="button" onClick={() => state.refetch()}>
                Try again
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <p>Loading your workspace</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  async function download(kind: "csv" | "json") {
    try {
      const blob = await api.download(`/rows.${kind}`);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `results.${kind}`;
      link.click();
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
      setToast(`Downloaded results.${kind}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setToast(error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : "The download failed. Try again.");
    }
  }

  async function startNewSession() {
    setConfirmNew(false);
    try {
      await api.post("/session/new");
      await refresh();
      setTab("chat");
    } catch (error) {
      setToast(error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : "Could not start a new session. Try again.");
    }
  }

  const rowCount = data.rows.length;
  return (
    <div className="page workspace">
      <TopBar person={person} usage={data.usage} canReset={!data.busy} onNewSession={() => setConfirmNew(true)} />
      <div className="work" data-tab={tab}>
        <div className="tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Workspace">
          <button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === "chat"} onClick={() => setTab("chat")}>
            Chat
          </button>
          <button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === "results"} onClick={() => setTab("results")}>
            Results ({rowCount})
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="pane chat">
          <div className="thread" aria-live="polite">
            {thread.length === 0 ? <EmptyThread examples={[]} /> : <Thread items={thread} />}
          </div>
        </div>
        <ResultsPane state={data} onDownload={download} />
      </div>
      <Dialog
        open={confirmNew}
        title="Start a new session?"
        onClose={() => setConfirmNew(false)}
        actions={
          <>
            <button className="btn" type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmNew(false)}>
              Keep working
            </button>
            <button className="btn btn-primary" type="button" onClick={startNewSession}>
              Clear everything
            </button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p>This clears the pages, results and chat, and any run that is waiting. Your settings stay.</p>
      </Dialog>
      {toast ? (
        <div className="toast" role="status">
          {toast}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 8: The conversation: send, watch it work, answer, stop, resume

The left half of the screen comes alive. This is the task a person feels, so its tests run whole journeys against the real stack with a scripted model.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/workspace/batch.ts`, `web/src/lib/workspace/run.ts`, `web/tests/workspace/batch.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/components/workspace/RunPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/workspace/Composer.tsx`, `web/src/components/workspace/Conversation.tsx`, `web/src/components/workspace/conversation.css`
- Create: `web/e2e/specs/conversation.spec.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/components/workspace/Workspace.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4's client, Task 5's reducer and derivations, Task 7's workspace, `Thread` and `Dialog`.
- Produces: `createBatcher(flush, schedule?)`; `useRun({ onNotice, onDrafts })` returning `{ live, start, ask, retry, answer, proceed, stop, discard, steer, clear }`; `<Conversation state run pendingDrafts onNotice>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `web/tests/workspace/batch.test.ts`**

A model streams text a word at a time, many times a second. Redrawing the whole conversation for each word is wasteful, so the frames are handed to the screen together, a few times a second.

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createBatcher } from "../../src/lib/workspace/batch";

function setup() {
  const flushed: number[][] = [];
  const timers: (() => void)[] = [];
  const batcher = createBatcher<number>((items) => flushed.push(items), (run) => timers.push(run));
  return { batcher, flushed, fire: () => timers.splice(0).forEach((run) => run()), timers };
}

describe("createBatcher", () => {
  it("hands over everything that arrived before the timer fired, in one go and in order", () => {
    const { batcher, flushed, fire } = setup();
    [1, 2, 3].forEach((n) => batcher.push(n));
    expect(flushed).toEqual([]);
    fire();
    expect(flushed).toEqual([[1, 2, 3]]);
  });

  it("sets one timer for a burst, and a new one for the next burst", () => {
    const { batcher, flushed, fire, timers } = setup();
    batcher.push(1);
    batcher.push(2);
    expect(timers).toHaveLength(1);
    fire();
    batcher.push(3);
    expect(timers).toHaveLength(1);
    fire();
    expect(flushed).toEqual([[1, 2], [3]]);
  });

  it("can be flushed at once, and the timer that was already set then finds nothing to do", () => {
    const { batcher, flushed, fire } = setup();
    batcher.push(1);
    batcher.flush();
    expect(flushed).toEqual([[1]]);
    fire();
    expect(flushed).toEqual([[1]]);
  });

  it("does nothing when flushed with nothing waiting", () => {
    const { batcher, flushed } = setup();
    batcher.flush();
    expect(flushed).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `web/src/lib/workspace/batch.ts`**

```
/** Collects items that arrive in a burst and hands them over together, so a fast stream of text redraws the screen a few times a second, not hundreds. */

const later = (run: () => void) => void setTimeout(run, 40);

export function createBatcher<T>(flush: (items: T[]) => void, schedule: (run: () => void) => void = later) {
  let waiting: T[] = [];
  let scheduled = false;
  const run = () => {
    scheduled = false;
    const items = waiting;
    waiting = [];
    if (items.length > 0) flush(items);
  };
  return {
    push(item: T) {
      waiting.push(item);
      if (!scheduled) {
        scheduled = true;
        schedule(run);
      }
    },
    flush: run,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `web/e2e/specs/conversation.spec.ts`**

These drive the real stack against the scripted model: a run that adds rows, a working indicator with Stop, stop and resume, a note sent while it works, a question with options and with free text, a daily limit refusal, a question about the pages with formatted answers (and hostile Markdown), the repeat limit card with a note, the new-site permission card, discarding a stopped run and getting back an unread note, a second tab, closing the page mid-run, and a reload.

```
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
import { env } from "../support/env";
import { fakeModelClient } from "../support/fake-model";
import { createMember, signInBrowser, type Member } from "../support/journeys";
import { PLAN_PAGES, PLAN_ROWS, seedSession, seedUsage, userIdOf } from "../support/seed";

const model = fakeModelClient(env.model);
test.beforeEach(() => model.reset());

async function signedIn(page: Page, prefix: string, seed?: Parameters<typeof seedSession>[1]): Promise<Member & { id: string }> {
  const member = await createMember(prefix);
  const id = await userIdOf(member.cookie);
  if (seed) await seedSession(id, seed);
  await signInBrowser(page.context(), member.cookie);
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Message")).toBeVisible();
  return { ...member, id };
}

const say = async (page: Page, text: string) => {
  await page.getByLabel("Message").fill(text);
  await page.getByLabel("Message").press("Enter");
};

const ADD_TWO = { name: "add_rows", args: { rows: [{ plan: "Solo", price: "$9" }, { plan: "Team", price: "$49" }] } };

test.describe("a run of the agent", () => {
  test("streams into the conversation, fills the results table and ends with a formatted answer", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }, { text: "I added two plans.\n\n**Solo** and **Team** are in the table.\n\n- Solo is $9\n- Team is $49" }]);
    await signedIn(page, "collect");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");

    await expect(page.locator(".msg-user", { hasText: "Collect the plans." })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("I added two plans.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator(".prose strong", { hasText: "Solo" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator(".prose li")).toHaveCount(2);
    await expect(page.getByText("Worked for 1 step")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText("2 rows added to the results table")).toBeVisible();

    const table = page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" });
    await expect(table.getByRole("row")).toHaveCount(3);
    await expect(table.getByRole("cell", { name: "Team", exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Message")).toHaveValue("");

    const [first] = await model.requests();
    expect(JSON.stringify(first.messages)).toContain("Collect the plans.");
    expect(first.tools).toContain("add_rows");
  });

  test("shows that it is working, with a stop button, until the model answers", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "answer", text: "Finished." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "working");
    await say(page, "Collect something.");
    const status = page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Step" });
    await expect(status.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "New session" })).toHaveCount(0);
    await model.release("answer");
    await expect(page.getByText("Finished.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test("stops on request, offers to resume, and carries on from where it was", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "first", toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }, { text: "All done after the pause." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "stopper");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }).click();
    await model.release("first");

    const stopped = page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "This run was stopped." });
    await expect(stopped).toBeVisible();
    await expect(stopped.getByRole("button", { name: "Discard this run" })).toBeVisible();
    await stopped.getByRole("button", { name: "Resume" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("All done after the pause.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("table", { name: "Collected results" }).getByRole("row")).toHaveCount(3);
  });

  test("a note sent while it works waits in the conversation until the agent reads it", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "first", toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }, { text: "Done, with your note in mind." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "steer");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send note" })).toBeDisabled();
    await page.getByLabel("Message").fill("Only the plans, please.");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send note" }).click();

    const bubble = page.locator(".msg-user.queued", { hasText: "Only the plans, please." });
    await expect(bubble).toBeVisible();
    await expect(bubble.getByText("Waiting for the agent to read this")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Message")).toHaveValue("");

    await model.release("first");
    await expect(page.getByText("Done, with your note in mind.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator(".msg-user.queued")).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(JSON.stringify((await model.requests()).at(-1)!.messages)).toContain("Only the plans, please.");
  });

  test("pauses with a question, and the answer goes back to the agent", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([
      { toolCalls: [{ name: "ask_user", args: { question: "Which price should I collect?", options: ["Monthly", "Yearly"] } }] },
      { text: "Collected the monthly price." },
    ]);
    await signedIn(page, "asker");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");

    const question = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Which price should I collect?" });
    await expect(question).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toHaveCount(0);
    await question.getByRole("button", { name: "Monthly" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Collected the monthly price.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(question).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(JSON.stringify((await model.requests()).at(-1)!.messages)).toContain("Monthly");
  });

  test("an answer typed in the person's own words works too", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ toolCalls: [{ name: "ask_user", args: { question: "Which price?", options: [] } }] }, { text: "Used your wording." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "words");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    const question = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Which price?" });
    await question.getByLabel("Your own answer").fill("Both, please");
    await question.getByRole("button", { name: "Send answer" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Used your wording.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(JSON.stringify((await model.requests()).at(-1)!.messages)).toContain("Both, please");
  });

  test("a person at the daily limit is told so, and keeps what they typed", async ({ page }) => {
    const member = await signedIn(page, "spent");
    await seedUsage(member.id, 300);
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await expect(page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: "Daily model-call limit reached" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Message")).toHaveValue("Collect the plans.");
    await expect(page.locator(".msg-user")).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(await model.requests()).toEqual([]);
  });
});

test.describe("a question about the pages", () => {
  test("streams reasoning and a formatted answer", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ reasoning: "Checking the Team page.", text: "The Team plan costs **$49** a month.\n\n| Plan | Price |\n| --- | --- |\n| Team | $49 |" }]);
    await signedIn(page, "ask", { pages: PLAN_PAGES, rows: PLAN_ROWS });
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Ask about the pages" }).click();
    await say(page, "What does Team cost?");
    await expect(page.locator(".msg-user", { hasText: "What does Team cost?" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator(".prose strong", { hasText: "$49" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator(".prose table")).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByText("Reasoning").click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Checking the Team page.")).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("shows only formatted text: no images, no scripts, no unsafe links", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([
      { text: "Hello ![tracker](http://evil.test/pixel.png) <img src=x onerror=alert(1)> [bad](javascript:alert(1)) [good](https://example.org/page).\n\n<script>window.hacked = true</script>" },
    ]);
    await signedIn(page, "unsafe", { pages: PLAN_PAGES });
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Ask about the pages" }).click();
    const requested: string[] = [];
    page.on("request", (request) => requested.push(request.url()));
    await say(page, "Say hello.");
    await expect(page.locator(".prose a", { hasText: "good" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "https://example.org/page");
    await expect(page.locator(".prose a", { hasText: "good" })).toHaveAttribute("rel", /noopener/);
    await expect(page.locator(".prose img")).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.locator(".prose a[href^='javascript']")).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.locator(".prose script")).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { hacked?: boolean }).hacked)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(requested.some((url) => url.includes("evil.test"))).toBe(false);
  });
});

test.describe("a run that needs a decision", () => {
  test("a repeated call asks what to do, and the note and choice go back to the agent", async ({ page }) => {
    const same = { toolCalls: [{ name: "add_rows", args: { rows: [{ plan: "Solo", price: "$9" }] } }] };
    await model.script([same, same, same, { text: "Wrapped up with what I had." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "limited");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");

    const card = page.getByRole("group", { name: /same call/ });
    for (const label of ["Continue", "Wrap up and answer", "Stop"]) await expect(card.getByRole("button", { name: label })).toBeVisible();
    await card.getByLabel("A note for the agent (optional)").fill("Keep it short.");
    await card.getByRole("button", { name: "Wrap up and answer" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Wrapped up with what I had.")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(card).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(JSON.stringify((await model.requests()).at(-1)!.messages)).toContain("Keep it short.");
  });

  test("a page on a new site asks for permission first, and a refusal is passed on", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ toolCalls: [{ name: "fetch_page", args: { url: "https://other.example/page" } }] }, { text: "I was not allowed to read other.example." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "site");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans from shop.example.");

    const card = page.getByRole("group", { name: "May I fetch pages from other.example?" });
    await expect(card.getByRole("button", { name: "Allow other.example" })).toBeVisible();
    await card.getByRole("button", { name: "Do not allow" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("I was not allowed to read other.example.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(await model.requests()).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test("discarding a stopped run clears it, and puts back a note that was never read", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "first", toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }]);
    await signedIn(page, "discard");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }).click();
    await page.getByLabel("Message").fill("Also the yearly price.");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send note" }).click();
    await expect(page.locator(".msg-user.queued", { hasText: "Also the yearly price." })).toBeVisible();
    await model.release("first");

    const stopped = page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "This run was stopped." });
    await stopped.getByRole("button", { name: "Discard this run" }).click();
    await expect(stopped).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "What should it collect?" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByLabel("Message")).toHaveValue("Also the yearly price.");
  });

  test("a second tab sees the run working, and can send it a note", async ({ page, context }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "first", toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }, { text: "Done, noted." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "twotabs");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeVisible();

    const other = await context.newPage();
    await other.goto("/");
    await expect(other.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Finishing up" })).toBeVisible();
    await other.getByLabel("Message").fill("Also the yearly price.");
    await other.getByRole("button", { name: "Send note" }).click();
    await expect(other.locator(".msg-user.queued", { hasText: "Also the yearly price." })).toBeVisible();

    await model.release("first");
    await expect(page.getByText("Done, noted.")).toBeVisible();
    await other.reload();
    await expect(other.getByText("Done, noted.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(JSON.stringify((await model.requests()).at(-1)!.messages)).toContain("Also the yearly price.");
  });

  test("closing the page in the middle of a run leaves a run that can be resumed", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "first", toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }, { text: "Finished after coming back." }]);
    await signedIn(page, "closer");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeVisible();
    await page.reload();
    await model.release("first");
    const card = page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "This run was stopped." });
    await expect(card.getByRole("button", { name: "Resume" })).toBeVisible();
    await card.getByRole("button", { name: "Resume" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Finished after coming back.")).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("a run stopped before the page was opened is still there after a reload", async ({ page }) => {
    await model.script([{ hold: "first", toolCalls: [ADD_TWO] }]);
    await signedIn(page, "reload");
    await say(page, "Collect the plans.");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }).click();
    await model.release("first");
    await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "This run was stopped." })).toBeVisible();
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page.locator(".msg-user", { hasText: "Collect the plans." })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "This run was stopped." }).getByRole("button", { name: "Resume" })).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```
