- [ ] **Step 7: Create `web/src/lib/workspace/run.ts`**

The one place that starts and follows runs. `stream` sends the request, applies each frame to the live turn (in batches), refreshes the results table a few times a second while the run works, and when the stream ends refreshes the saved state and decides whether the live turn stays (a failure stays, because the saved state does not say why it failed). A refusal before anything started comes back to the caller, so the person's text is put back. Leaving the page aborts the stream, which stops the run on the server and leaves it resumable.

```
"use client";

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useReducer, useRef } from "react";
import { useApi } from "../../components/workspace/ApiProvider";
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";
import type { SseFrame } from "../api/sse";
import type { RunView, WorkspaceState } from "../api/types";
import { createBatcher } from "./batch";
import { shouldKeepLive } from "./derive";
import { CONNECTION_LOST, liveReducer, type LiveAction, type LiveTurn } from "./live";
import { STATE_KEY, useRefreshState } from "./state";

export type SteerOutcome = "queued" | "refused";

const STEER_REFUSALS: Record<string, string> = {
  full: "The agent has not read your earlier messages yet. Wait a moment, then send this again.",
  too_long: "Keep follow-up messages under 2000 characters.",
  empty: "Write something to send.",
  no_run: "There is no run to send this to.",
};

const messageOf = (error: unknown) => (error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : "Something went wrong. Try again.");

/** Starts and follows the agent's runs and questions, feeding what streams back into one live turn. */
export function useRun(options: { onNotice: (text: string) => void; onDrafts: (drafts: string[]) => void }) {
  const api = useApi();
  const queries = useQueryClient();
  const refresh = useRefreshState();
  const [live, dispatch] = useReducer(liveReducer, null);
  const liveRef = useRef<LiveTurn | null>(null);
  liveRef.current = live;
  const abort = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const optionsRef = useRef(options);
  optionsRef.current = options;

  useEffect(() => () => abort.current?.abort(), []); // leaving the page stops the run on the server, which keeps it resumable

  const stream = useCallback(
    /** Resolves to the server's reason when it refused to start the request at all, so the caller can put the person's text back. */
    async (path: string, body: unknown, begin: LiveAction): Promise<string | null> => {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      abort.current = controller;
      // `mirror` follows the same actions the screen gets, so what the turn ended as is known here without waiting for a render.
      let mirror = liveReducer(liveRef.current, begin);
      dispatch(begin);
      const batcher = createBatcher<LiveAction>((actions) => actions.forEach(dispatch));
      // While the run works, the results table and the page list follow along, a few times a second at most.
      let refreshTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
      const refreshSoon = () => {
        if (!refreshTimer) refreshTimer = setTimeout(() => ((refreshTimer = null), void refresh()), 400);
      };
      const apply = (action: LiveAction) => {
        mirror = liveReducer(mirror, action);
        batcher.push(action);
        if (action.type === "frame" && (action.event === "rows_added" || action.event === "tool_result")) refreshSoon();
      };
      let refused: string | null = null;
      let sawFrame = false;
      try {
        for await (const frame of api.stream(path, body, controller.signal) as AsyncIterable<SseFrame>) {
          sawFrame = true;
          apply({ type: "frame", event: frame.event, data: frame.data });
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return null;
        const broken = !(error instanceof ApiError); // the stream began and then broke, or could not be read
        if (!broken && !sawFrame) refused = messageOf(error);
        apply({ type: "failed", message: broken ? CONNECTION_LOST : messageOf(error), resumable: broken });
      }
      batcher.flush();
      if (refreshTimer) clearTimeout(refreshTimer);
      if (abort.current === controller) abort.current = null;
      if (controller.signal.aborted || !mirror) return null;
      if (refused && begin.type === "start" && !begin.from) {
        dispatch({ type: "clear" }); // nothing began, so nothing is left to show: the caller keeps the person's text and says why
        return refused;
      }

      const turn = mirror;
      await refresh();
      if (!shouldKeepLive(turn)) dispatch({ type: "clear" });
      if (turn.kind === "ask" && turn.status === "error" && turn.notice) optionsRef.current.onNotice(turn.notice);
      if (turn.drafts.length) optionsRef.current.onDrafts(turn.drafts);
      if (turn.leftovers.length && turn.status !== "paused") {
        // The run ended before the agent read these messages: they become a normal turn.
        const state = queries.getQueryData<WorkspaceState>(STATE_KEY);
        if (state?.model) void stream("/agent/run", { instruction: turn.leftovers.join("\n"), model: state.model }, { type: "start", kind: "agent", text: turn.leftovers.join("\n") });
      }
      return refused;
    },
    [api, queries, refresh],
  );

  const continuing = (run: RunView | null): LiveAction => {
    const current = liveRef.current;
    if (current && current.kind === "agent" && current.status !== "streaming") return { type: "resume" };
    return { type: "start", kind: "agent", text: run?.instruction ?? "", from: run ?? undefined };
  };

  return {
    live,
    start: (text: string, model: string, seedUrl?: string) =>
      stream("/agent/run", { instruction: text, model, seed_url: seedUrl || undefined }, { type: "start", kind: "agent", text }),
    clear: () => dispatch({ type: "clear" }),
    ask: (text: string, model: string) => stream("/ask", { question: text, model }, { type: "start", kind: "ask", text }),
    retry: (text: string, model: string) => stream("/ask/retry", { model }, { type: "start", kind: "ask", text }),
    answer: (value: string | { action?: string; note?: string; allow?: boolean }, run: RunView | null) => stream("/agent/resume", { value }, continuing(run)),
    proceed: (run: RunView | null) => stream("/agent/proceed", undefined, continuing(run)),
    async stop() {
      try {
        await api.post("/agent/stop");
      } catch (error) {
        optionsRef.current.onNotice(messageOf(error));
      }
    },
    async discard() {
      try {
        const { drafts } = await api.post<{ drafts: string[] }>("/agent/discard");
        dispatch({ type: "clear" });
        await refresh();
        if (drafts.length) optionsRef.current.onDrafts(drafts);
      } catch (error) {
        optionsRef.current.onNotice(messageOf(error));
      }
    },
    async steer(text: string): Promise<SteerOutcome> {
      try {
        const result = await api.post<{ status: string; id: string }>("/agent/steer", { text });
        if (result.status !== "queued") {
          optionsRef.current.onNotice(STEER_REFUSALS[result.status] ?? "That message could not be sent.");
          return "refused";
        }
        if (liveRef.current) dispatch({ type: "queued", id: result.id, text });
        else await refresh(); // a run this screen is not streaming: the saved state lists the message as waiting
        return "queued";
      } catch (error) {
        optionsRef.current.onNotice(messageOf(error));
        return "refused";
      }
    },
  };
}

export type RunController = ReturnType<typeof useRun>;
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `web/src/components/workspace/RunPanel.tsx`**

What the run needs from the person: nothing (it is working, with Stop), an answer (an option or free text; a limit decision with an optional note; permission for a new site), or a decision to resume or discard.

```
"use client";

import { useId, useState } from "react";
import type { LimitPending, Pending } from "../../lib/api/types";
import type { RunPanel as Panel } from "../../lib/workspace/derive";

type Answer = string | { action?: string; note?: string; allow?: boolean };
type Handlers = { onAnswer: (value: Answer) => void; onDiscard: () => void };

function AskCard({ pending, onAnswer }: { pending: Extract<Pending, { kind: "ask" }>; onAnswer: (value: Answer) => void }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const inputId = useId();
  return (
    <>
      {pending.options.length ? (
        <div className="options">
          {pending.options.map((option) => (
            <button key={option} className="btn" type="button" onClick={() => onAnswer(option)}>
              {option}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <form
        className="free"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          if (text.trim()) onAnswer(text.trim());
        }}
      >
        <label className="sr" htmlFor={inputId}>
          Your own answer
        </label>
        <input id={inputId} type="text" value={text} onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)} placeholder={pending.options.length ? "Or answer in your own words" : "Your answer"} />
        <button className="btn" type="submit" disabled={!text.trim()}>
          Send answer
        </button>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}

function LimitCard({ pending, onAnswer }: { pending: LimitPending; onAnswer: (value: Answer) => void }) {
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const inputId = useId();
  return (
    <>
      <div className="free">
        <label className="sr" htmlFor={inputId}>
          A note for the agent (optional)
        </label>
        <input id={inputId} type="text" value={note} onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)} placeholder="Add a note for the agent (optional)" />
      </div>
      <div className="options">
        {pending.actions.map((action) => (
          <button key={action.action} className={action.action === "continue" ? "btn btn-primary" : "btn"} type="button" onClick={() => onAnswer({ action: action.action, note: note.trim() })}>
            {action.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function QuestionCard({ pending, onAnswer, onDiscard }: { pending: Pending } & Handlers) {
  const labelId = useId();
  return (
    <div className="question" role="group" aria-labelledby={labelId}>
      <p className="q" id={labelId}>
        {pending.question}
      </p>
      {pending.kind === "ask" ? <AskCard pending={pending} onAnswer={onAnswer} /> : null}
      {pending.kind === "limit" ? <LimitCard pending={pending} onAnswer={onAnswer} /> : null}
      {pending.kind === "site" ? (
        <div className="options">
          <button className="btn btn-primary" type="button" onClick={() => onAnswer({ allow: true })}>
            Allow {pending.host}
          </button>
          <button className="btn" type="button" onClick={() => onAnswer({ allow: false })}>
            Do not allow
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <div>
        <button className="btn btn-quiet btn-small" type="button" onClick={onDiscard}>
          Discard this run
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** What the run needs from the person right now: nothing (it is working), an answer, or a decision to resume or discard. */
export function RunPanel({ panel, pagesRead, onStop, onResume, onAnswer, onDiscard }: { panel: Panel; pagesRead: number; onStop: () => void; onResume: () => void } & Handlers) {
  if (panel.kind === "none") return null;
  if (panel.kind === "question") return <QuestionCard pending={panel.pending} onAnswer={onAnswer} onDiscard={onDiscard} />;
  if (panel.kind === "working") {
    return (
      <div className="live" role="status">
        <div className="line">
          <span className="pulse" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span className="grow">{panel.doing}</span>
          <button className="btn btn-small" type="button" onClick={onStop}>
            Stop
          </button>
        </div>
        <span className="muted small">
          {panel.step > 0 ? `Step ${panel.step}` : "Starting"}
          {pagesRead > 0 ? `, ${pagesRead} ${pagesRead === 1 ? "page" : "pages"} read` : ""}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="live stopped" role="status">
      <div className="line">
        <span className="pulse" aria-hidden="true" />
        <span className="grow">{panel.message}</span>
        {panel.resumable ? (
          <button className="btn btn-small btn-primary" type="button" onClick={onResume}>
            Resume
          </button>
        ) : null}
        <button className="btn btn-small" type="button" onClick={onDiscard}>
          Discard this run
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Composer.tsx`**

Collect or Ask about the pages, a message box (Enter sends, Shift+Enter adds a line), the page to start from, the model, and Send. While a run works, the same box sends a note to the agent. In Ask mode a page can be read first, optionally with the pages it links to.

```
"use client";

import type { FormEvent, KeyboardEvent, RefObject } from "react";
import type { ModelView } from "../../lib/api/types";

export type Mode = "collect" | "ask";

export type ComposerProps = {
  textRef: RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>;
  text: string;
  onText: (value: string) => void;
  mode: Mode;
  onMode: (mode: Mode) => void;
  url: string;
  onUrl: (value: string) => void;
  onUrlCommit: () => void;
  follow: boolean;
  onFollow: (value: boolean) => void;
  depth: number;
  onDepth: (value: number) => void;
  models: ModelView[];
  model: string | null;
  onModel: (id: string) => void;
  modelsError: string | null;
  /** Why the last message was refused, such as a daily limit. */
  problem: string | null;
  working: boolean;
  reading: boolean;
  /** The agent has a run going, so what is typed goes to it as a note. */
  steering: boolean;
  onSend: () => void;
  onReadPage: () => void;
  onCancelRead: () => void;
};

export function Composer(p: ComposerProps) {
  const canSend = p.text.trim().length > 0 && p.model !== null && (p.mode === "collect" || !p.working);
  const placeholder =
    p.mode === "ask"
      ? p.working
        ? "A run is working. Switch to Collect to send it a note."
        : "Ask a question about the pages it has read"
      : p.steering
        ? "Send a note to the agent while it works"
        : "Tell it what to collect";

  function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (canSend) p.onSend();
  }
  function keyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) {
    if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey && !event.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
      event.preventDefault();
      if (canSend) p.onSend();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="composer-wrap">
      <form className="composer" onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="row">
          <div className="seg" role="group" aria-label="What to do">
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={p.mode === "collect"} onClick={() => p.onMode("collect")}>
              Collect
            </button>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={p.mode === "ask"} onClick={() => p.onMode("ask")}>
              Ask about the pages
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <label className="sr" htmlFor="composer-text">
          Message
        </label>
        <textarea id="composer-text" ref={p.textRef} rows={2} value={p.text} placeholder={placeholder} onChange={(event) => p.onText(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={keyDown} />
        <div className="row">
          <label className="field-inline" htmlFor="start-url">
            {p.mode === "ask" ? "Page" : "Start from"}
            <input id="start-url" type="text" inputMode="url" value={p.url} placeholder="Optional: a page address" onChange={(event) => p.onUrl(event.target.value)} onBlur={p.onUrlCommit} />
          </label>
          {p.mode === "ask" ? (
            <>
              <label className="field-inline" htmlFor="read-follow">
                <input id="read-follow" type="checkbox" checked={p.follow} onChange={(event) => p.onFollow(event.target.checked)} />
                Also read linked pages
              </label>
              {p.follow ? (
                <label className="field-inline" htmlFor="read-depth">
                  Depth
                  <select id="read-depth" value={p.depth} onChange={(event) => p.onDepth(Number(event.target.value))}>
                    {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
                      <option key={n} value={n}>
                        {n}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>
              ) : null}
              <button className="btn btn-small" type="button" disabled={p.reading ? false : !p.url.trim() || p.working} onClick={p.reading ? p.onCancelRead : p.onReadPage}>
                {p.reading ? "Cancel" : "Read page"}
              </button>
            </>
          ) : null}
          <span className="grow" />
          <label className="sr" htmlFor="model">
            Model
          </label>
          <select id="model" value={p.model ?? ""} disabled={p.models.length === 0} onChange={(event) => p.onModel(event.target.value)}>
            {p.models.length === 0 ? <option value="">No model available</option> : null}
            {p.models.map((model) => (
              <option key={model.id} value={model.id}>
                {model.id}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button className="btn btn-primary" type="submit" disabled={!canSend}>
            {p.mode === "collect" && p.steering ? "Send note" : "Send"}
          </button>
        </div>
        {p.problem || p.modelsError ? (
          <p className="notice" role="alert">
            {p.problem ?? p.modelsError}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Create `web/src/components/workspace/Conversation.tsx`**

```
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ApiError } from "../../lib/api/client";
import type { WorkspaceState } from "../../lib/api/types";
import { deriveRunPanel, deriveThread, isWorking } from "../../lib/workspace/derive";
import type { RunController } from "../../lib/workspace/run";
import { useApi } from "./ApiProvider";
import { Composer, type Mode } from "./Composer";
import { RunPanel } from "./RunPanel";
import { EmptyThread, Thread } from "./Thread";
import "./conversation.css";
import { useRefreshState } from "../../lib/workspace/state";

const EXAMPLES = [
  "Collect every plan and price from shop.example/plans.",
  "List the title and date of each post on blog.example, newest first.",
  "Find the support email on example.org and say which page it is on.",
];

/** How far from the bottom still counts as "following" the conversation. */
const FOLLOW_PX = 80;

export function Conversation({ state, run, pendingDrafts, onNotice }: { state: WorkspaceState; run: RunController; pendingDrafts: string[]; onNotice: (text: string) => void }) {
  const api = useApi();
  const refresh = useRefreshState();
  const { live } = run;
  const textRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  const threadRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const following = useRef(true);

  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>(state.settings.agent === "0" ? "ask" : "collect");
  const [url, setUrl] = useState(state.settings.url);
  const [follow, setFollow] = useState(state.settings.follow === "1");
  const [depth, setDepth] = useState(Math.min(3, Math.max(1, Number(state.settings.depth) || 1)));
  const [reading, setReading] = useState(false);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const model = state.models.some((m) => m.id === state.model) ? state.model : (state.models[0]?.id ?? null);
  const working = isWorking(state, live);
  const items = useMemo(() => deriveThread(state, live), [state, live]);
  const panel = deriveRunPanel(state, live);
  const pagesRead = live ? live.log.filter((entry) => entry.name === "fetch_page" && entry.ok).length : 0;
  const lastMessage = state.history.at(-1);
  const retryable = !live && !working && lastMessage?.role === "assistant" && lastMessage.interrupted;

  // Messages the server handed back (typed after Stop, or never read) return to the box, ahead of anything typed since.
  const drafts = pendingDrafts.join("\n");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!drafts) return;
    setText((current) => (current.includes(drafts) ? current : current ? `${drafts}\n${current}` : drafts));
    textRef.current?.focus();
  }, [drafts]);

  // Follow the newest text, unless the person has scrolled up to read.
  const newest = `${items.length}:${live?.answer.length ?? 0}:${live?.reasoning.length ?? 0}:${live?.log.length ?? 0}:${panel.kind}`;
  useEffect(() => {
    const thread = threadRef.current;
    if (thread && following.current) thread.scrollTop = thread.scrollHeight;
  }, [newest]);

  const save = (name: string, value: string) => api.put(`/settings/${name}`, { value }).catch(() => undefined);

  function pickMode(next: Mode) {
    setMode(next);
    void save("agent", next === "collect" ? "1" : "0");
  }

  async function send() {
    const message = text.trim();
    if (!message || !model) return;
    setProblem(null);
    if (mode !== "ask" && (working || panel.kind === "question")) {
      if ((await run.steer(message)) === "queued") setText("");
      return;
    }
    setText("");
    const refused = mode === "ask" ? await run.ask(message, model) : await run.start(message, model, url.trim() || undefined);
    if (refused) {
      setText((current) => current || message);
      setProblem(refused);
    }
  }

  async function readPage() {
    setReading(true);
    try {
      await api.post("/scrape", { url: url.trim(), mode: "fast", follow, depth });
      await refresh();
    } catch (error) {
      const hint = error instanceof ApiError && typeof error.extra.hint === "string" ? ` ${error.extra.hint}` : "";
      onNotice(error instanceof ApiError ? `${error.message}${hint}` : "The page could not be read. Try again.");
    } finally {
      setReading(false);
    }
  }

  async function chooseModel(id: string) {
    await api.put("/model", { id }).catch((error) => onNotice(error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : "Could not switch models."));
    await refresh();
  }

  return (
    <div className="pane chat">
      <div
        className="thread"
        ref={threadRef}
        aria-live="polite"
        onScroll={(event) => {
          const el = event.currentTarget;
          following.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < FOLLOW_PX;
        }}
      >
        {items.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyThread
            examples={EXAMPLES}
            onPick={(example) => {
              setText(example);
              textRef.current?.focus();
            }}
          />
        ) : (
          <Thread items={items} onRetry={retryable && model ? () => void run.retry(state.history.at(-2)?.content ?? "", model) : undefined} />
        )}
      </div>
      <RunPanel
        panel={panel}
        pagesRead={pagesRead}
        onStop={() => void run.stop()}
        onResume={() => void run.proceed(state.run)}
        onAnswer={(value) => void run.answer(value, state.run)}
        onDiscard={() => void run.discard()}
      />
      <Composer
        textRef={textRef}
        text={text}
        onText={setText}
        mode={mode}
        onMode={pickMode}
        url={url}
        onUrl={setUrl}
        onUrlCommit={() => void save("url", url.trim())}
        follow={follow}
        onFollow={(value) => {
          setFollow(value);
          void save("follow", value ? "1" : "0");
        }}
        depth={depth}
        onDepth={(value) => {
          setDepth(value);
          void save("depth", String(value));
        }}
        models={state.models}
        model={model}
        onModel={(id) => void chooseModel(id)}
        modelsError={state.models_error}
        problem={problem}
        working={working}
        reading={reading}
        steering={working || panel.kind === "question"}
        onSend={() => void send()}
        onReadPage={() => void readPage()}
        onCancelRead={() => void api.post("/scrape/cancel").catch(() => undefined)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Create `web/src/components/workspace/conversation.css`**

```
.live { display: grid; gap: 0.5rem; border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; padding: 0.7rem 0.9rem; background: var(--surface); }
.live .line { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.6rem; }
.live .pulse { width: 0.55rem; height: 0.55rem; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--accent); animation: pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite; flex: none; }
.live.stopped .pulse { animation: none; background: var(--muted); }
.live .grow { flex: 1; min-width: 0; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
@keyframes pulse { 50% { opacity: 0.25; } }

.question {
  width: min(100% - 2.5rem, 44rem); margin: 0 auto 0.75rem; padding: 0.9rem 1rem; border: 1.5px solid var(--accent); border-radius: 10px; background: var(--surface);
  display: grid; gap: 0.7rem; box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}
.question p.q { font-family: var(--font-read); font-size: var(--step-1); line-height: 1.5; }
.question .options, .composer .row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.5rem; }
.question .free { display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; }
.question .free input { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }

.composer-wrap { padding: 0 1.25rem 1.1rem; }
.composer {
  width: min(100%, 44rem); margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 12px; background: var(--surface); padding: 0.6rem 0.7rem 0.7rem;
  display: grid; gap: 0.5rem;
}
.composer:focus-within { border-color: var(--accent); }
.composer textarea { width: 100%; resize: none; border: 0; background: transparent; padding: 0.3rem 0.2rem; min-height: 3.2rem; outline: none; font-family: var(--font-read); font-size: var(--step-1); line-height: 1.5; }
.composer .row { align-items: center; }
.composer .row .grow { flex: 1; }
.seg { display: inline-flex; background: var(--sunken); border-radius: 8px; padding: 2px; }
.seg button { border: 0; background: transparent; padding: 0.2rem 0.7rem; border-radius: 6px; font-size: var(--step--1); font-weight: 560; color: var(--muted); }
.seg button[aria-pressed="true"] { background: var(--surface); color: var(--ink); box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12); }
.field-inline { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.4rem; font-size: var(--step--1); color: var(--muted); }
.field-inline input[type="text"] { width: 15rem; max-width: 100%; }
select { padding-right: 1.6rem; }

.live.stopped .line { flex-wrap: wrap; }
.composer input[type="checkbox"] { width: 1rem; height: 1rem; accent-color: var(--accent); }
.composer .notice { padding: 0.5rem 0.7rem; border-radius: 8px; background: var(--bad-soft); font-size: var(--step--1); }
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .composer-wrap { padding-inline: 1rem; }
  .question { width: calc(100% - 2rem); }
  .field-inline input[type="text"] { width: 100%; }
  .field-inline { flex: 1 1 100%; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .live .pulse { animation: none; } }
```

### Task 9: The administrator's page

Invitations and people, in the design of the mock. It is the page that makes the service invite-only in practice.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/admin-view.ts`, `web/tests/admin-view.test.ts`, `web/src/components/admin/AdminPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/admin/admin.css`, `web/src/app/admin/page.tsx`, `web/e2e/specs/admin.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 2's administrator routes (`/api/admin/invites`) and Better Auth's admin client (`listUsers`, `banUser`, `unbanUser`, `removeUser`); Task 7's `Dialog`; Task 3's `requireAdmin`.
- Produces: `inviteStatusLabel`, `roleLabel`, `personStatus`, `formatDay`; `<AdminPanel invites people selfId>`; the `/admin` page.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `web/tests/admin-view.test.ts`**

Dates are written in UTC with a fixed format, so the server and the browser print the same text.

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatDay, inviteStatusLabel, personStatus, roleLabel } from "../src/lib/admin-view";

describe("inviteStatusLabel", () => {
  it("uses the words people use for each state", () => {
    expect(inviteStatusLabel("pending")).toBe("Waiting");
    expect(inviteStatusLabel("used")).toBe("Joined");
    expect(inviteStatusLabel("revoked")).toBe("Revoked");
    expect(inviteStatusLabel("expired")).toBe("Expired");
  });
});

describe("roleLabel", () => {
  it("calls an administrator an administrator and everyone else a member", () => {
    expect(roleLabel("admin")).toBe("Administrator");
    expect(roleLabel("user")).toBe("Member");
    expect(roleLabel(null)).toBe("Member");
    expect(roleLabel(undefined)).toBe("Member");
  });
});

describe("personStatus", () => {
  it("is Banned only for a banned person", () => {
    expect(personStatus({ banned: true })).toBe("Banned");
    expect(personStatus({ banned: false })).toBe("Active");
    expect(personStatus({ banned: null })).toBe("Active");
  });
});

describe("formatDay", () => {
  it("writes the same short date on the server and in the browser, whatever the time zone", () => {
    expect(formatDay("2026-10-10T23:30:00.000Z")).toBe("Oct 10, 2026");
    expect(formatDay("2026-01-02T00:00:00.000Z")).toBe("Jan 2, 2026");
  });
  it("is empty for something that is not a date", () => {
    expect(formatDay("nonsense")).toBe("");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `web/src/lib/admin-view.ts`**

```
/** The words and dates on the administrator's page. */
import type { InviteStatus } from "./invites";

const STATUS: Record<InviteStatus, string> = { pending: "Waiting", used: "Joined", revoked: "Revoked", expired: "Expired" };

export const inviteStatusLabel = (status: InviteStatus): string => STATUS[status];
export const roleLabel = (role: string | null | undefined): string => (role === "admin" ? "Administrator" : "Member");
export const personStatus = (person: { banned?: boolean | null }): "Banned" | "Active" => (person.banned ? "Banned" : "Active");

const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { year: "numeric", month: "short", day: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" });

/** `Oct 10, 2026`, in UTC, so the server and the browser print the same thing. */
export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "" : DAY.format(date);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `web/e2e/specs/admin.spec.ts`**

```
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import pg from "pg";
import { env } from "../support/env";
import { createMember, emailLink, logLength, ownerCookie, signInBrowser, signInHttp, uniqueEmail } from "../support/journeys";
import { PLAN_ROWS, seedSession, userIdOf } from "../support/seed";

test.describe("the administrator's page", () => {
  test("is for administrators only", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("plain");
    await signInBrowser(context, member.cookie);
    await page.goto("/admin");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/$/);
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Invitations and people" })).toHaveCount(0);

    await context.clearCookies();
    await page.goto("/admin");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sign-in/);
  });

  test("invites someone, who gets the email, and revokes the invitation, then invites again", async ({ page, context }) => {
    await signInBrowser(context, await ownerCookie());
    await page.goto("/admin");
    const email = uniqueEmail("guest");
    const since = logLength();

    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(email);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send invitation" }).click();
    const row = page.getByRole("row", { name: new RegExp(email) });
    await expect(row.getByText("Waiting")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: `Invitation sent to ${email}` })).toBeVisible();
    expect(await emailLink(email, "You are invited to Page Chat", since)).toContain("/sign-up#invite=");

    await row.getByRole("button", { name: "Revoke" }).click();
    await expect(row.getByText("Revoked")).toBeVisible();

    await row.getByRole("button", { name: "Invite again" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("row", { name: new RegExp(email) }).getByText("Waiting")).toHaveCount(1);
  });

  test("says so when the address already has an account, or is not an address", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("taken");
    await signInBrowser(context, await ownerCookie());
    await page.goto("/admin");
    await page.getByLabel("Email address").fill(member.email);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send invitation" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("alert").filter({ hasText: "That address already has an account." })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test("bans someone, who can then no longer use the service, and lifts the ban", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("banned");
    await signInBrowser(context, await ownerCookie());
    await page.goto("/admin");
    const row = page.getByRole("region", { name: "People" }).getByRole("row", { name: new RegExp(member.email) });
    await row.getByRole("button", { name: "Ban" }).click();
    await expect(row.locator(".pill", { hasText: "Banned" })).toBeVisible();
    expect((await fetch(`${env.web}/api/auth/token`, { headers: { cookie: member.cookie } })).status).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(400);

    await row.getByRole("button", { name: "Lift ban" }).click();
    await expect(row.locator(".pill", { hasText: "Active" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(signInHttp(member.email)).resolves.toContain("=");
  });

  test("removes someone after asking, and erases what the agent saved for them", async ({ page, context }) => {
    const member = await createMember("removed");
    const id = await userIdOf(member.cookie);
    await seedSession(id, { rows: PLAN_ROWS });
    await signInBrowser(context, await ownerCookie());
    await page.goto("/admin");
    const row = page.getByRole("region", { name: "People" }).getByRole("row", { name: new RegExp(member.email) });

    await row.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove" }).click();
    const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: /Remove/ });
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Keep this account" }).click();
    await expect(row).toBeVisible();

    await row.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove" }).click();
    await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove account" }).click();
    await expect(row).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Account removed" })).toBeVisible();

    const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: env.database });
    await client.connect();
    const left = await client.query("SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM app.rows WHERE user_id = $1) AS rows", [id]);
    await client.end();
    expect(Number(left.rows[0].rows)).toBe(0);
  });

  test("offers no ban or removal for the administrator's own row", async ({ page, context }) => {
    await signInBrowser(context, await ownerCookie());
    await page.goto("/admin");
    const own = page.getByRole("region", { name: "People" }).getByRole("row", { name: /owner@example.com/ }).filter({ hasText: "Administrator" });
    await expect(own.getByText("You")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(own.getByRole("button")).toHaveCount(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Create `web/src/components/admin/AdminPanel.tsx`**

Invite someone (the address, then Send invitation), the invitations with Revoke and Invite again, and the people with Ban, Lift ban and Remove. Removing someone asks first, and erases what the agent saved for them. Nobody gets buttons on their own row.

```
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { authClient } from "../../lib/auth-client";
import { formatDay, inviteStatusLabel, personStatus, roleLabel } from "../../lib/admin-view";
import type { InviteStatus } from "../../lib/invites";
import { Dialog } from "../Dialog";
import "./admin.css";

export type InviteRow = { id: string; email: string; status: InviteStatus; createdAt: string };
export type PersonRow = { id: string; name: string; email: string; role: string | null; banned: boolean };

const PILL: Record<InviteStatus, string> = { pending: "waiting", used: "used", revoked: "revoked", expired: "expired" };

async function invite(email: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const response = await fetch("/api/admin/invites", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
  if (response.ok) return null;
  const body = (await response.json().catch(() => null)) as { error?: { message?: string } } | null;
  return body?.error?.message ?? "The invitation could not be sent. Try again.";
}

export function AdminPanel({ invites, people, selfId }: { invites: InviteRow[]; people: PersonRow[]; selfId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [removing, setRemoving] = useState<PersonRow | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3200);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  async function act(work: () => Promise<string | null>, done: string) {
    setBusy(true);
    const failure = await work().catch(() => "Something went wrong. Try again.");
    setBusy(false);
    if (failure) setToast(failure);
    else {
      setToast(done);
      router.refresh();
    }
  }

  async function send(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setProblem(null);
    const failure = await invite(email.trim()).catch(() => "Something went wrong. Try again.");
    setBusy(false);
    if (failure) return setProblem(failure);
    setToast(`Invitation sent to ${email.trim()}`);
    setEmail("");
    router.refresh();
  }

  const apiError = (result: { error: { message?: string } | null }) => (result.error ? (result.error.message ?? "That did not work. Try again.") : null);

  return (
    <div className="admin">
      <div className="admin-inner">
        <h1>Invitations and people</h1>

        <section aria-labelledby="invite-heading">
          <h2 id="invite-heading">Invite someone</h2>
          <form className="invite-form" onSubmit={send}>
            <label htmlFor="invite-email">
              Email address
              <input id="invite-email" type="email" placeholder="name@example.com" required value={email} onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)} />
            </label>
            <button className="btn btn-primary" type="submit" disabled={busy}>
              Send invitation
            </button>
          </form>
          {problem ? (
            <p className="error-box" role="alert">
              {problem}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          <p className="muted small">The invitation works once, only for this address, and expires after 7 days.</p>
        </section>

        <section aria-labelledby="invitations-heading">
          <h2 id="invitations-heading">Invitations</h2>
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className="plain">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">Address</th>
                  <th scope="col">Status</th>
                  <th scope="col">Sent</th>
                  <th scope="col">
                    <span className="sr">Actions</span>
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {invites.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={4} className="muted">
                      No invitations yet.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : null}
                {invites.map((row) => (
                  <tr key={row.id}>
                    <td>{row.email}</td>
                    <td>
                      <span className={`pill ${PILL[row.status]}`}>{inviteStatusLabel(row.status)}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td>{formatDay(row.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td className="act">
                      {row.status === "pending" ? (
                        <button
                          className="btn btn-small"
                          type="button"
                          disabled={busy}
                          onClick={() =>
                            act(async () => {
                              const response = await fetch(`/api/admin/invites/${row.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
                              return response.ok ? null : "The invitation could not be revoked. Try again.";
                            }, "Invitation revoked")
                          }
                        >
                          Revoke
                        </button>
                      ) : null}
                      {row.status === "expired" || row.status === "revoked" ? (
                        <button className="btn btn-small" type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => act(() => invite(row.email), `Invitation sent to ${row.email}`)}>
                          Invite again
                        </button>
                      ) : null}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section aria-labelledby="people-heading">
          <h2 id="people-heading">People</h2>
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className="plain">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">Name</th>
                  <th scope="col">Address</th>
                  <th scope="col">Role</th>
                  <th scope="col">Status</th>
                  <th scope="col">
                    <span className="sr">Actions</span>
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {people.map((person) => (
                  <tr key={person.id}>
                    <td>{person.name}</td>
                    <td>{person.email}</td>
                    <td>{roleLabel(person.role)}</td>
                    <td>
                      <span className={`pill ${person.banned ? "banned" : "active"}`}>{personStatus(person)}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="act">
                      {person.id === selfId ? (
                        <span className="muted small">You</span>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <button
                            className="btn btn-small"
                            type="button"
                            disabled={busy}
                            onClick={() =>
                              act(
                                async () => apiError(person.banned ? await authClient.admin.unbanUser({ userId: person.id }) : await authClient.admin.banUser({ userId: person.id })),
                                person.banned ? `${person.name} can sign in again` : `${person.name} is banned`,
                              )
                            }
                          >
                            {person.banned ? "Lift ban" : "Ban"}
                          </button>{" "}
                          <button className="btn btn-small btn-danger" type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setRemoving(person)}>
                            Remove
                          </button>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      <Dialog
        open={removing !== null}
        title={`Remove ${removing?.name ?? "this person"}?`}
        onClose={() => setRemoving(null)}
        actions={
          <>
            <button className="btn" type="button" onClick={() => setRemoving(null)}>
              Keep this account
            </button>
            <button
              className="btn btn-danger"
              type="button"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={() => {
                const person = removing;
                setRemoving(null);
                if (person) void act(async () => apiError(await authClient.admin.removeUser({ userId: person.id })), "Account removed, and its saved data erased");
              }}
            >
              Remove account
            </button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p>
          {removing?.email}'s account, results and everything the agent saved for them are erased and cannot be recovered.
        </p>
      </Dialog>

      {toast ? (
        <div className="toast" role="status">
          {toast}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Create `web/src/app/admin/page.tsx`**

The invitations and the people are read on the server, together, and passed down. After a change, the panel asks the server to render the page again.

```
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { AdminPanel, type InviteRow, type PersonRow } from "../../components/admin/AdminPanel";
import { getServices } from "../../lib/services";
import { requireAdmin } from "../../lib/session";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Invitations and people" };

export default async function AdminPage() {
  const session = await requireAdmin();
  const { invites, auth } = getServices();
  const [waiting, listed] = await Promise.all([invites.list(), auth.api.listUsers({ headers: await headers(), query: { limit: 200, sortBy: "createdAt", sortDirection: "desc" } })]);

  const inviteRows: InviteRow[] = waiting.map((invite) => ({ id: invite.id, email: invite.email, status: invite.status, createdAt: invite.createdAt.toISOString() }));
  const people: PersonRow[] = listed.users.map((user) => ({ id: user.id, name: user.name, email: user.email, role: user.role ?? null, banned: user.banned === true }));
  return (
    <div className="page">
      <header className="topbar">
        <Link className="wordmark" href="/">
          Page Chat
        </Link>
        <Link className="btn btn-quiet" href="/">
          Back to workspace
        </Link>
      </header>
      <AdminPanel invites={inviteRows} people={people} selfId={session.user.id} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `web/src/components/workspace/DeleteAccountDialog.tsx`**

It asks for the password again, so a borrowed screen cannot delete the account, and says plainly when the password is wrong. A deleted account goes to the sign-in page with a note.

```
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useId, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { authClient } from "../../lib/auth-client";
import { describeAuthError } from "../../lib/auth-errors";
import { Dialog } from "../Dialog";

/** Erases the account and everything saved for it. Asks for the password again, so a borrowed screen cannot do it. */
export function DeleteAccountDialog({ open, onClose }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const formId = useId();
  const fieldId = useId();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      setPassword("");
      setProblem(null);
    }
  }, [open]);

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPending(true);
    setProblem(null);
    const { error } = await authClient.deleteUser({ password });
    if (error) {
      setProblem(describeAuthError(error, "delete"));
      setPending(false);
      return;
    }
    router.replace("/sign-in?deleted=1");
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      title="Delete your account?"
      onClose={onClose}
      actions={
        <>
          <button className="btn" type="button" onClick={onClose}>
            Keep my account
          </button>
          <button className="btn btn-danger" type="submit" form={formId} disabled={pending || !password}>
            Delete my account
          </button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <p>Your account, results and everything the agent saved for you are erased and cannot be recovered. Enter your password to confirm.</p>
      <form className="form" id={formId} onSubmit={submit}>
        <label htmlFor={fieldId}>
          Password
          <input id={fieldId} type="password" autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)} />
        </label>
        {problem ? (
          <p className="error-box" role="alert">
            {problem}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

### Task 11: Remove NiceGUI and write the new README

The Next.js app is the only interface now. The repository never had a stretch with no working interface: this is the last task.

**Files:**
- Delete: `src/scraper/app.py`, `src/scraper/theme.py`, `src/scraper/__main__.py`, `tests/test_app.py`, `tests/test_main.py`, `conftest.py`
- Create: `tests/test_no_nicegui.py`
- Modify: `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock`, `README.md`, `.env.example`, `web/README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: A working web app (Tasks 3 to 10).
- Produces: No NiceGUI anywhere; `python -m scraper` no longer exists (the API is `python -m scraper_api`); a README for the hosted service.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create `tests/test_no_nicegui.py`**

The old interface is removed for good, and this test keeps it removed: no dependency, no import, none of the old modules, and no pytest plugin that only NiceGUI's tests needed.

```python
"""The NiceGUI interface is gone for good: the Next.js app in web/ is the only interface."""

import importlib.util
import tomllib
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


def test_the_project_no_longer_depends_on_nicegui():
    project = tomllib.loads((ROOT / "pyproject.toml").read_text())
    assert not [d for d in project["project"]["dependencies"] if "nicegui" in d.lower()]
    assert "main_file" not in project["tool"]["pytest"]["ini_options"]  # a setting only NiceGUI's test plugin reads


def test_no_source_file_mentions_nicegui():
    offenders = [str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) for p in (ROOT / "src").rglob("*.py") if "nicegui" in p.read_text().lower()]
    assert offenders == []


def test_the_old_interface_modules_are_gone():
    for name in ("scraper.app", "scraper.theme", "scraper.__main__"):
        assert importlib.util.find_spec(name) is None, f"{name} should have been removed"


def test_no_pytest_plugin_for_nicegui_is_loaded():
    assert not (ROOT / "conftest.py").exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Create `README.md`**

The README described the local single-user app. It now describes the hosted service, how it is built, how to run all of it on one machine against a local model, and what is sent where.

````markdown
# Page Chat

Tell a model in plain English what to collect from a website. It fetches the pages with [Scrapling](https://github.com/D4Vinci/Scrapling), reads them, asks you when something is unclear, and hands back an answer plus a table you can download.

Page Chat is a hosted, invite-only service. An administrator invites people by email; each person has their own account, session, results and daily allowance, kept apart from everyone else's.

## What it does

- **Tell it what to collect.** "Go to shop.example/plans and collect every plan with its price." Say how, too: "use the `.price` selector", "only the /products pages".
- **It asks when it is stuck.** The agent pauses with a question when your instruction is ambiguous, a login or CAPTCHA blocks a page, or a choice changes the outcome. It also pauses when it hits its step, page or repeat limit (continue, wrap up, or stop) and before it fetches a site you did not name (allow or do not allow).
- **Talk to it while it works.** Send a note ("skip the blog pages", "also grab the SKU") and the agent reads it before its next step, keeping what it has gathered. The note shows as waiting until it is read. Press Stop and anything unread goes back into the box.
- **Nothing is lost.** Your session (pages, results, conversation) and any paused, stopped or interrupted run are saved. Close the tab, come back, and press Resume or answer the question that was waiting.
- **Results you can use.** Collected rows appear in a table, in the column order the agent chose, with CSV and JSON download.
- **Ask about the pages.** Switch to "Ask about the pages" to read a page yourself and ask questions about it, with no tools. Use this for models without tool calling.

## How it is built

| Part | Where | Does |
|---|---|---|
| Web app | `web/` (Next.js, Better Auth) | The screens, accounts, email confirmation, invitations, and the short-lived tokens the browser sends to the API |
| API | `src/scraper_api/` (FastAPI) | One live session per signed-in person, runs streamed to the browser as server-sent events, daily limits |
| Agent library | `src/scraper/` | The agent, its tools, fetching, the guards against reaching private networks, the model clients |
| Database | Postgres | A schema `auth` for the web app and a schema `app` for the API, so the API cannot read password hashes |

## Run it on your machine

You need [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) with Python 3.14, Node 20.9 or later, Postgres, and a model server. For development use [LM Studio](https://lmstudio.ai/) with a chat model that supports tool calling (a Ministral 3 8B build works), so no page text leaves your machine and nothing is billed.

1. Make a database and read [docs/hosted-deploy.md](docs/hosted-deploy.md) for the two roles used in production. For a first run, one database and one user are enough.
2. Start the API, from a directory of your choice:

   ```bash
   SCRAPER_HOSTED=true SCRAPER_PROVIDER=lmstudio SCRAPER_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:1234/v1 \
   SCRAPER_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user@localhost/scraper \
   SCRAPER_JWKS_URL=http://localhost:3000/api/auth/jwks SCRAPER_JWT_ISSUER=http://localhost:3000 SCRAPER_JWT_AUDIENCE=http://localhost:8080 \
   SCRAPER_SERVICE_SECRET=<a long random string> SCRAPER_CORS_ORIGINS=http://localhost:3000 \
   uv run python -m scraper_api
   ```

3. Start the web app, with the matching settings in `web/.env.local` (copy `web/.env.example`; the table in [docs/hosted-deploy.md](docs/hosted-deploy.md) lists which setting pairs with which):

   ```bash
   cd web && npm install && npm run migrate && npm run dev
   ```

   With `EMAIL_TRANSPORT=log` (the development default) invitation and confirmation links are printed in the terminal running `npm run dev`.
4. Open http://localhost:3000 and sign up with the address in `ADMIN_EMAIL`. That first account is the administrator and needs no invitation; everyone after it does.

`compose.yaml` runs Postgres and the API in containers (`podman-compose up --build`); the web app runs separately.

## Instruction cookbook

| You want | Say something like |
|---|---|
| A table of items | "Go to https://shop.example/products, open each product page, and collect name, price and url." |
| A specific selector | "On https://example.com/list use the selector `.row .title` and give me the first 20 titles." |
| To be consulted | "Find the contact email for the support team. Ask me if there is more than one." |
| A follow-up | In "Ask about the pages": "Which of those costs less than 50 dollars?" (it reuses the pages already read) |
| To change course mid-run | While it works, send: "Only the cheapest plan, please." |

The agent may fetch only sites you named in the instruction, the "Start from" address, or pages already read. Anything else asks you first.

## Privacy and safety

- **With the hosted Mistral provider, the text of every page the model reads is sent to Mistral.** With a local model server, it goes only to that server. The API turns LangSmith and other cloud tracing off at start-up.
- Page text and prompts are never written to a log.
- The API refuses addresses on private networks, and the host must also block them at the network level: see [docs/hosted-deploy.md](docs/hosted-deploy.md), section 3.
- Scraped pages are untrusted: the agent is told never to follow instructions found inside them, and its allowlist and step budgets limit what a hostile page could make it do. That reduces the risk of prompt injection but does not remove it.
- The agent's answers are shown as formatted text only: no images, no scripts, and only ordinary links.

## Configuration

The API reads environment variables (`SCRAPER_<NAME>`), or a `.env` file in the directory it runs from; real variables win over the file. The settings for a hosted deployment are in [docs/hosted-deploy.md](docs/hosted-deploy.md). These tune the agent:

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `SCRAPER_AGENT_MAX_STEPS` | `25` | Model steps per round before the agent pauses to ask |
| `SCRAPER_AGENT_MAX_PAGES` | `30` | Page fetches per round before it pauses to ask |
| `SCRAPER_TOOL_RESULT_TOKENS` | `3000` | Size cap for each tool result |
| `SCRAPER_FALLBACK_CONTEXT` | `16384` | Context size assumed when the model does not report one |
| `SCRAPER_CONTEXT_FRACTION` / `SCRAPER_REPLY_RESERVE` / `SCRAPER_HISTORY_RESERVE` | `0.7` / `2048` / `3000` | How much of the context window each part of a prompt may use |
| `SCRAPER_CRAWL_MAX_PAGES` / `SCRAPER_CRAWL_DEPTH` / `SCRAPER_REQUEST_DELAY` | `20` / `1` / `0.5` | Limits for reading linked pages. `SCRAPER_REQUEST_DELAY` is also the minimum pause between the agent's fetches: the model can raise it with `set_fetch_delay` (up to 30s) when a site pushes back, but never go below it |
| `SCRAPER_FETCH_TIMEOUT` | `20` | Seconds per page |

## Tests

```bash
uv run pytest
```

The Postgres tests are skipped unless `SCRAPER_TEST_DATABASE_URL` points at a database they may fill and empty (use a scratch one). A run with it unset still reports green, so check the summary says `0 skipped` before you trust it:

```bash
podman run -d --name scraper-test-db -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=test -e POSTGRES_DB=scraper_test -p 127.0.0.1:55432:5432 docker.io/library/postgres:17
export SCRAPER_TEST_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres:test@127.0.0.1:55432/scraper_test
uv run pytest -rs
```

The web app has its own tests, and two end-to-end checks that start the real servers: see [web/README.md](web/README.md).
````

```markdown
- Deleting an account first asks the Python API to erase that person's data; if it cannot, the account stays and the deletion can be retried.
```

```markdown
- Deleting an account first asks the Python API to erase that person's data; if it cannot, the account stays and the deletion can be retried.
- The screens read the API's address from `API_URL` on the server and hand it to the browser, so there is no build-time setting for it. The browser calls the API directly (a token in the `Authorization` header), because a run streams for minutes and would hit Vercel's function time limit if proxied.
- The agent's answer is rendered with `react-markdown` loaded on demand, with raw HTML, images and non-web links turned off, because the text can quote any web page.
```

## Self-review notes

- **Spec coverage:** the sign-in, sign-up, forgot and reset password, verify-email screens (Task 3); the API client with the cached token, a retry on `401`, and streaming with `fetch` (Task 4); the live run as a reducer fed by the stream, server state through TanStack Query (Tasks 5, 7, 8); the pause cards (ask with options, limit with a note, new site), queued-message bubbles, stop, resume and discard (Task 8); the results table with CSV and JSON download, the pages read, the daily allowance meter, the "pages are sent to" notice is not shown for a local model and is a README statement for hosted Mistral (Tasks 7 and 11); the administrator page (Task 9); deleting your account (Task 10); NiceGUI removed (Task 11).
- **Not built, by decision:** a visible "pages are sent to Mistral" banner in the screen (the spec asks for one; the approved mock has none, and the README states it; add it in the design pass), a theme switch, saved-session history, and removal of the SQLite store.
- **Verified while writing:** every code block came from a scratch copy where its tests passed; the unit tests were watched failing first; the browser tests ran against the real web app, the real Python API and a scripted model (no live model was used at any point). Several protections were broken on purpose to see a test fail. The plan was then replayed step by step on a fresh checkout.
- **Not verified:** the container image (the Podman machine was unavailable), email through Resend, a deployment on Vercel, a real model through LM Studio (do one manual run with a local model before launch), and the screens in Safari and Firefox (only Chromium ran).
- **Known gap to fix before a public launch:** plan 1's note on the follow-up delivery race (a flaky test `test_a_follow_up_is_not_injected_twice_when_the_delivered_flag_is_lost_in_a_crash`) is unchanged.
